.github/workflows/release.yml

Requires a tag matching VERSION, reuses tests, builds final artifacts, attests their origin, verifies hashes/provenance and creates a draft release.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (88 lines)
name: Verified release builds
on:
  push:
    tags: ['v*']
  workflow_dispatch:
permissions:
  contents: read
concurrency:
  group: release-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: false
jobs:
  validate:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
      - name: Require a version tag matching the source
        env:
          RELEASE_REF: ${{ github.ref }}
        run: |
          version=$(tr -d '\r\n' < VERSION)
          test "$RELEASE_REF" = "refs/tags/v$version"
  checks:
    needs: validate
    uses: ./.github/workflows/test.yml
    permissions:
      contents: read
  build:
    needs: checks
    runs-on: windows-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      id-token: write
      attestations: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
      - name: Build tested release artifacts
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          ./Build-Release.ps1 -PackageOnly
          ./Build-Linux.ps1
      - name: Attest final downloadable binaries
        uses: actions/attest@1e69f48acb82d1966a394da916b4c1698aa569d6 # v4
        with:
          subject-path: |
            dist/*.exe
            dist/*.zip
            dist/*.sh
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: verified-release
          path: dist/*
          if-no-files-found: error
  draft:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: write
      attestations: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@9000827ccba6bdab643e8b6fd33ac0654aef8333 # v8
        with:
          name: verified-release
          path: dist
      - name: Verify provenance and prepare a draft release
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          GH_REPO: ${{ github.repository }}
          RELEASE_TAG: ${{ github.ref_name }}
        run: |
          for artifact in dist/*.exe dist/*.zip dist/*.sh; do
            gh attestation verify "$artifact" --repo "$GH_REPO"
          done
          cd dist
          sha256sum -c ./*.sha256
          cd ..
          cat > notes.md <<'NOTES'
          Download LMS-Unpacker.exe for the Windows desktop app, LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe for the independent Windows CLI, or LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh for Linux (requires PowerShell 7).

          Defaults skip existing filenames and permanently delete ZIPs after successful processing, including skips. Preview first; choose Keep ZIP to retain archives.

          These artifacts were built and tested in GitHub Actions. Check the SHA-256 sidecars and verify provenance with `gh attestation verify <download> --repo Ahmed-Asif1/lms-unpacker`.

          Windows artifacts are currently unsigned with Authenticode. Build attestations verify origin; they are not an antivirus or Windows trust certification. See docs/SIGNING.md in the repository for the code signing policy.

          We are preparing a SignPath Foundation application; no approval or SignPath-signed release is claimed. [Code signing policy](https://github.com/Ahmed-Asif1/lms-unpacker/blob/main/docs/SIGNING.md) · [Privacy policy](https://github.com/Ahmed-Asif1/lms-unpacker/blob/main/PRIVACY.md) · [Download guide](https://github.com/Ahmed-Asif1/lms-unpacker/blob/main/docs/DOWNLOADS.md).
          NOTES
          gh release create "$RELEASE_TAG" --verify-tag --draft --title "LMS Unpacker $RELEASE_TAG" --notes-file notes.md dist/*

.github/workflows/test.yml

Runs Windows engine and desktop tests, Linux portable/shell checks and guide checks; packages outputs only after prerequisite jobs pass.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (65 lines)
name: Test and package
on:
  push:
    branches: ['**']
  pull_request:
  workflow_call:
permissions:
  contents: read
jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: windows-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
      - name: Windows PowerShell 5.1 integration tests
        shell: powershell
        run: ./tests/Run-Tests.ps1
      - name: PowerShell 7 integration tests
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./tests/Run-Tests.ps1
      - name: Desktop control flows
        shell: powershell
        run: powershell.exe -NoProfile -STA -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ./Start-Desktop.ps1 -SmokeTest
      - name: Portable engine and profile checks
        shell: powershell
        run: ./tests/Test-Portable.ps1
      - name: Generated-clutter cleanup checks
        shell: powershell
        run: ./tests/Test-Cleanup.ps1
      - name: Keep source reference current
        shell: powershell
        run: |
          ./tools/Build-CodeReference.ps1
          git diff --exit-code -- docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html
  linux:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
      - name: Portable engine tests on Linux
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./tests/Test-Portable.ps1
      - name: Offline visual guide checks
        run: node tests/Test-Guide.js
      - name: Build standalone Linux launcher
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./Build-Linux.ps1
      - name: Test standalone shell launcher
        shell: bash
        run: bash tests/Test-Linux.sh
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: LMS-Unpacker-Linux
          path: dist/*
  package:
    needs: [test, linux]
    runs-on: windows-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1
      - name: Build portable release
        shell: pwsh
        run: ./Build-Release.ps1 -PackageOnly
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2
        with:
          name: LMS-Unpacker
          path: dist/*

Build-Linux.ps1

PowerShell 7 build entry point. Zips the explicit CLI runtime list in memory, appends Base64 to a Bash wrapper and writes a checksum. Does not bundle pwsh.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (22 lines)
#requires -Version 7.0
[CmdletBinding()]
param([string]$OutputPath=(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'dist/LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh'))
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$output=$ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OutputPath)
$null=[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($output))
$memory=[IO.MemoryStream]::new()
$zip=[IO.Compression.ZipArchive]::new($memory,[IO.Compression.ZipArchiveMode]::Create,$true)
try {
    foreach ($file in @('Start-Cli.ps1','Start-Unpacker.ps1','Unpack-Lms.ps1','LmsUnpacker.psm1','ZipIntegrity.cs','Undo-Organization.ps1','New-CourseProfile.ps1','START-HERE.html','README.md','PRIVACY.md','CONTRIBUTING.md','docs/DOWNLOADS.md','docs/ROADMAP.md','docs/SIGNPATH-APPLICATION.md','SECURITY.md','LICENSE','VERSION','profiles/nust.json','profiles/courses.example.csv','profiles/syllabus.example.csv','docs/PROFILES.md','docs/SIGNING.md','docs/BUILD-IT-YOURSELF.html','docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html')) {
        $null=[IO.Compression.ZipFileExtensions]::CreateEntryFromFile($zip,(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot $file),$file)
    }
} finally { $zip.Dispose() }
try {
    $launcher=([IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'packaging/LinuxLauncher.sh')) -replace "`r`n","`n").TrimEnd() + "`n"
    $payload=[Convert]::ToBase64String($memory.ToArray(),[Base64FormattingOptions]::InsertLineBreaks) -replace "`r`n","`n"
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($output,($launcher + $payload + "`n"),[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    $hash=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $output -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText(($output+'.sha256'),($hash+'  '+[IO.Path]::GetFileName($output)+"`n"))
    Write-Host "Linux launcher: $output"
} finally { $memory.Dispose() }

Build-Release.ps1

Windows build entry point. Runs tests, generates icon, creates an explicit runtime ZIP, compiles desktop/CLI hosts, tests standalone outputs and writes SHA-256 sidecars. Refuses an existing versioned ZIP.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (77 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
[CmdletBinding()]
param([switch]$PackageOnly)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$buildRoot = $null
Push-Location $PSScriptRoot
try {
    if (-not $PackageOnly) {
        & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'tests\Run-Tests.ps1')
        $windowsPowerShell = Join-Path $env:WINDIR 'System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe'
        & $windowsPowerShell -NoProfile -STA -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Start-Desktop.ps1') -SmokeTest
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Desktop flow tests failed.' }
    }
    $version = (Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'VERSION') -Raw).Trim()
    if ($version -notmatch '^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { throw 'Invalid VERSION.' }
    $dist = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'dist'
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($dist)
    $releaseDestination = Join-Path $dist "LMS-Unpacker-$version.zip"
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $releaseDestination) { throw "Release already exists: $releaseDestination. Move it elsewhere before rebuilding." }
    $buildRoot = Join-Path $dist ('.build-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($buildRoot)
    $destination = Join-Path $buildRoot "LMS-Unpacker-$version.zip"
    $icon = Join-Path $buildRoot 'App.ico'
    & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'packaging\New-AppIcon.ps1') -OutputPath $icon
    $files = @('Unpack-Lms.ps1','LmsUnpacker.psm1','ZipIntegrity.cs','Start-Unpacker.ps1','Run-Unpack.bat','Run-Unpack.sh','Start-Cli.ps1','Start-Desktop.ps1','Desktop.xaml','DesktopIntegration.psm1','DesktopFeatures.ps1','ProfileEditor.ps1','HistoryDialog.ps1','DesktopFeedback.ps1','DesktopPaths.psm1','DesktopDiscovery.ps1',
        'Undo-Organization.ps1','New-CourseProfile.ps1','START-HERE.html','LAUNCH-HELP.txt','README.md','PRIVACY.md','CONTRIBUTING.md','docs/DOWNLOADS.md','docs/ROADMAP.md','docs/SIGNPATH-APPLICATION.md','SECURITY.md','LICENSE','VERSION','CHANGELOG.md',
        'profiles\nust.json','profiles\courses.example.csv','profiles\syllabus.example.csv','docs\PROFILES.md','docs\SIGNING.md','docs\BUILD-IT-YOURSELF.html','docs\CODE-REFERENCE.html')
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    $zip = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($destination,'Create')
    try {
        foreach ($file in $files) {
            $null = [IO.Compression.ZipFileExtensions]::CreateEntryFromFile($zip,(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot $file),('LMS-Unpacker/' + $file.Replace('\','/')))
        }
        $null = [IO.Compression.ZipFileExtensions]::CreateEntryFromFile($zip,$icon,'LMS-Unpacker/App.ico')
    } finally { $zip.Dispose() }
    $compiler = Join-Path $env:WINDIR 'Microsoft.NET\Framework64\v4.0.30319\csc.exe'
    if (-not [IO.File]::Exists($compiler)) { $compiler = Join-Path $env:WINDIR 'Microsoft.NET\Framework\v4.0.30319\csc.exe' }
    $common = @('/nologo','/optimize+','/platform:anycpu',('/resource:' + $destination + ',App.zip'),('/win32icon:' + $icon),
        ('/win32manifest:' + (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'packaging\App.manifest')),
        '/r:System.IO.Compression.dll','/r:System.IO.Compression.FileSystem.dll','/r:System.Windows.Forms.dll','/r:System.Web.Extensions.dll')
    $desktop = Join-Path $buildRoot 'LMS-Unpacker.exe'
    $cli = Join-Path $buildRoot 'LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe'
    foreach ($edition in @(@{Path=$desktop; Type='/target:winexe'; Define='/define:DESKTOP'},@{Path=$cli; Type='/target:exe'; Define='/define:CLI'})) {
        & $compiler @common $edition.Type $edition.Define ('/out:' + $edition.Path) (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'packaging\PortableHost.cs')
        if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Portable executable compilation failed.' }
    }
    & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'tests\Test-Release.ps1') -CliPath $cli -DesktopPath $desktop
    foreach ($artifact in @($desktop,$cli,$destination)) {
        $hash = (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $artifact -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText(($artifact + '.sha256'),($hash + '  ' + [IO.Path]::GetFileName($artifact) + [Environment]::NewLine))
        foreach ($file in @($artifact,($artifact + '.sha256'))) {
            $publishPath = Join-Path $dist ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($file))
            for ($attempt = 0; ; $attempt++) {
                try {
                    if ([IO.File]::Exists($publishPath)) { [IO.File]::Replace($file,$publishPath,[NullString]::Value) }
                    else { [IO.File]::Move($file,$publishPath) }
                    break
                } catch [IO.IOException] {
                    if ($attempt -ge 5) { throw }
                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds (200 * ($attempt + 1))
                }
            }
        }
        Write-Host ('Release: ' + (Join-Path $dist ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($artifact))))
        Write-Host "SHA256: $hash"
    }
} finally {
    if ($buildRoot -and [IO.Directory]::Exists($buildRoot)) {
        $resolvedBuild = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($buildRoot)
        $distPrefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'dist')).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
        if ($resolvedBuild.StartsWith($distPrefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -and [IO.Path]::GetFileName($resolvedBuild) -like '.build-*') {
            Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolvedBuild -Recurse -Force
        }
    }
    Pop-Location
}

DesktopDiscovery.ps1

Dot-sourced UI helper: tooltip descriptions, independent picker memory, startup/discovery requests, a debounced background scanner and stale-result suppression.

Set-DesktopHelp

Builds the named-control help dictionary, creates explicitly styled wrapping ToolTips, adds AutomationProperties.HelpText and configures delay/duration/disabled-control help. UI-only, called after named controls exist.

Declared at line 1

Get-WorkspaceHint

Input: saved workspace. Produces a multiline summary of folder, queue count, task, output and retention for item hover help. Does not touch documents.

Declared at line 62

Update-WorkspaceHints

Adds transient Hint fields to in-memory saved workspaces, falling back to a readable message for invalid data. Hints are omitted from persisted preferences.

Declared at line 68

Remember-PickerSelection

Inputs: Folder/Zip kind and accepted path. Derives a directory, updates only that picker memory and saves preferences outside SmokeTest. Selection alone is enough to remember a location.

Declared at line 74

New-InputFolderDialog

Creates a WinForms FolderBrowserDialog with the independent remembered/launch starting folder and no new-folder action. Caller shows and disposes it; this function does not show a window.

Declared at line 79

New-InputZipDialog

Creates a multiselect WPF OpenFileDialog restricted to ZIPs and seeded with the independent ZIP-folder memory. Caller handles accepted selection.

Declared at line 86

Request-ZipDiscovery

Inputs: optional startup candidates and Force. Increments request version, snapshots current paths/depth and queues a debounced scan. SmokeTest suppresses incidental scans unless forced. Does not perform extraction.

Declared at line 92

Read-DiscoveryResult

Input: current-version worker result. Optionally changes startup selection under a reentrancy guard, binds discovered rows/counts and updates scope help. Selects the archive tab only if no plan is currently shown and no work is active.

Declared at line 99
Read the full source (170 lines)
function Set-DesktopHelp {
    $help=@{
        HelpButton='Open the offline visual guide: first launch, scan depth, workspaces, conflicts, course rules and recovery.'
        WorkspaceExpander='A workspace remembers paths, queue, profile and task settings. It does not copy documents. Save one for a semester or a regular routine.'
        WorkspaceChoice='Load a saved setup. Hover a saved item to inspect its folder and settings before choosing it.'
        WorkspaceName='Name this setup, for example Fall semester. Saving an existing name updates it.'
        SaveWorkspaceButton='Save the current paths and task settings locally for reuse next time.'
        RemoveWorkspaceButton='Forget the selected workspace. Your documents are not deleted.'
        QueueExpander='Process several independent folders or ZIPs in order. A single Courses folder already scans its immediate course folders.'
        QueueList='Select an additional path to reorder or remove it. Delete removes only the queue entry, not the file or folder.'
        QueueAddButton='Keep the current path in the queue, then choose the next folder or ZIP.'
        QueueClearButton='Clear additional queued paths. Keep the main selection and all files on disk.'
        QueueFirstButton='Swap this queued path with the main path. Under Skip, the first matching destination wins.'
        QueueRemoveButton='Remove this path from the queue without deleting it from disk.'
        QueueUpButton='Move the selected input earlier. At the top, swap it with the main input.'
        QueueDownButton='Move the selected input later in the queue.'
        OptionsExpander='Change filename conflicts, one-level course-folder scanning and the optional ZIP output folder.'
        AppExpander='Optional Start menu shortcut installation and removal.'
        FolderButton='Choose the folder containing your ZIPs or course folders. First use starts beside the app; later choices remember the last folder.'
        ZipButton='Choose one or several ZIPs with Ctrl or Shift. First use starts beside the app; later choices remember the last ZIP folder.'
        AppFolderButton='Select the folder containing this EXE. Useful when you keep the app beside your course folders.'
        RescanButton='Refresh the ZIP list after adding downloads. Discovery reads filenames only; Preview checks archive contents.'
        TaskChoice='Flatten removes LMS wrapper folders. Just unzip keeps the original structure. Organization adds optional course categories and syllabus rules.'
        KeepZip='Unchecked by default: successfully processed ZIPs are permanently deleted, including skipped incoming files. Check this to retain archives.'
        ScanChildren='Scan this folder and immediate course folders (one level). Deeper folders, links, hidden folders and known working folders such as Lab and submission are excluded.'
        ConflictChoice='Skip keeps existing names even when content differs. Rename keeps different versions with numbered names. Identical matching content is skipped.'
        OutputPath='Leave blank to extract beside each ZIP. A chosen destination collects ZIP output; course routing and original folder structure still apply.'
        OutputBrowseButton='Choose where ZIP contents are written. Loose documents are always organized in place with undo.'
        ProfileChoice='General works with any courses. The NUST mapping is optional. Choose your own JSON profile to use your course codes and syllabus.'
        ProfilePath='Path to your own validated JSON course profile.'
        ProfileBrowseButton='Load your own course names and filename rules from a JSON profile.'
        EditProfileButton='Edit course names and syllabus rules visually. Save a new profile copy to preserve the original.'
        CreateProfileButton='Create a course profile from a courses CSV and optional syllabus CSV. Example columns are explained in the visual guide.'
        GroupChoice='Group lectures by category, known week or named topic. Uncertain week information is never guessed.'
        ClassifyButton='Choose an organization task, preview, and select a file to create a reusable classification rule.'
        UnclassifiedOnly='Show only files without a confident category. Loose documents stay unchanged; unmatched ZIP entries are extracted into the selected course or ZIP output folder.'
        UndoButton='Find previous organization records, then preview restoring loose documents. Extraction and deleted ZIPs cannot be undone here.'
        PreviewButton='Read and validate files, then show proposed actions. Preview never extracts, moves or deletes your materials.'
        ApplyButton='Run a successful preview first. Apply rechecks the files and follows your conflict and ZIP deletion settings.'
        CancelButton='Stop at the next safe boundary. Completed work remains; unprocessed ZIPs are retained.'
        OpenFolderButton='Open the selected folder, or ZIP output destination, in File Explorer.'
        ResultOpenButton='Open the result folder. For multiple input locations this opens the first result folder.'
        ArchivesTab='ZIP filenames found in the current scope. Discovery does not open or extract the archives.'
        ArchiveGrid='Found ZIPs. Select one or more rows, then Use selected ZIPs to limit the next preview.'
        UseDetectedButton='Limit processing to the selected ZIPs. Ctrl or Shift selects several. Preview again after changing the selection.'
        StartMenuButton='Copy the EXE to your local Programs folder and create a per-user Start shortcut. No administrator access is needed.'
        RemoveShortcutButton='Remove the Start shortcut. The copied app remains in your local Programs folder.'
    }
    foreach ($name in $help.Keys) {
        # Popup content lives outside the window's visual tree. Give it the
        # wrapping template explicitly, instead of relying on inherited text properties.
        $tip=[Windows.Controls.ToolTip]::new()
        $tip.Style=$window.FindResource([Windows.Controls.ToolTip])
        $tip.Content=$help[$name]
        $ui[$name].ToolTip=$tip
        [Windows.Automation.AutomationProperties]::SetHelpText($ui[$name],$help[$name])
        [Windows.Controls.ToolTipService]::SetInitialShowDelay($ui[$name],350)
        [Windows.Controls.ToolTipService]::SetShowDuration($ui[$name],20000)
        [Windows.Controls.ToolTipService]::SetShowOnDisabled($ui[$name],$true)
    }
}
function Get-WorkspaceHint($Saved) {
    $task=@('Flatten ZIPs','Just unzip','Organize ZIPs and documents','Organize documents only')[[int]$Saved.Task]
    $output=if ($Saved.Output) { $Saved.Output } else { 'Beside each ZIP' }
    $retention=if ($Saved.KeepZip) { 'Keep ZIPs' } else { 'Delete successful ZIPs' }
    return "Folder: $($Saved.Path)`nAdditional inputs: $(@($Saved.Queue).Count)`nTask: $task`nOutput: $output`n$retention"
}
function Update-WorkspaceHints {
    foreach ($saved in $state.Workspaces) {
        $hint=try { Get-WorkspaceHint $saved } catch { 'Saved setup. Remove and recreate it if the settings are no longer valid.' }
        $saved | Add-Member -NotePropertyName Hint -NotePropertyValue $hint -Force
    }
}
function Remember-PickerSelection([string]$Kind,[string]$Selected) {
    $folder=if ([IO.Directory]::Exists($Selected)) { $Selected } else { [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($Selected) }
    if ($Kind -eq 'Folder') { $state.LastFolderPicker=$folder } else { $state.LastZipPicker=$folder }
    if (-not $SmokeTest) { Save-Preferences }
}
function New-InputFolderDialog {
    $dialog=[Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog]::new()
    $dialog.Description='Choose the folder containing your ZIPs or course folders'
    $dialog.ShowNewFolderButton=$false
    $dialog.SelectedPath=Get-LmsPickerFolder $state.LastFolderPicker $state.LaunchFolder
    return $dialog
}
function New-InputZipDialog {
    $dialog=[Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog]::new()
    $dialog.Title='Choose ZIPs'; $dialog.Filter='ZIP archives (*.zip)|*.zip'; $dialog.Multiselect=$true
    $dialog.InitialDirectory=Get-LmsPickerFolder $state.LastZipPicker $state.LaunchFolder
    return $dialog
}
function Request-ZipDiscovery([string[]]$StartupCandidates,[switch]$Force) {
    if ($SmokeTest -and -not $Force) { return }
    $state.DiscoveryVersion++
    $state.DiscoveryPending=@{Paths=@($ui.InputPath.Text)+@($ui.QueueList.Items); Depth=[int][bool]$ui.ScanChildren.IsChecked; Startup=@($StartupCandidates); Version=$state.DiscoveryVersion}
    $ui.DiscoveryText.Text='Looking for ZIPs...'
    $discoveryTimer.Start()
}
function Read-DiscoveryResult($Result) {
    if ($Result.SelectedPath -and $Result.SelectedPath -ne $ui.InputPath.Text) {
        $state.UpdatingDiscovery=$true
        try { $ui.InputPath.Text=$Result.SelectedPath } finally { $state.UpdatingDiscovery=$false }
    }
    $ui.ArchiveGrid.ItemsSource=@($Result.Rows)
    $count=@($Result.Rows).Count
    $ui.ArchivesTab.Header="ZIPs found ($count)"
    $scope=if ($ui.ScanChildren.IsChecked) { 'this folder and one level of course folders' } else { 'the selected folder only' }
    if ($ui.QueueList.Items.Count) { $scope='the selected inputs' }
    $ui.DiscoveryText.Text=if ($count) { "$count ZIP(s) found in $scope. Preview to check their contents." } else { "No ZIPs found in $scope. Choose another location or organize existing documents." }
    if (-not $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource -and -not $state.Busy) { $ui.ResultsTabs.SelectedIndex=3 }
}
$discoveryTimer=[Windows.Threading.DispatcherTimer]::new()
$discoveryTimer.Interval=[TimeSpan]::FromMilliseconds(300)
$discoveryTimer.Add_Tick({
    try {
        if ($state.DiscoveryWorker) {
            if (-not $state.DiscoveryHandle.IsCompleted) { return }
            try {
                $output=@($state.DiscoveryWorker.EndInvoke($state.DiscoveryHandle))
                if ($state.DiscoveryActiveVersion -eq $state.DiscoveryVersion -and -not $state.Busy) {
                    if ($state.DiscoveryWorker.HadErrors) { throw 'Could not scan every selected folder. Check access permissions and try Rescan.' }
                    Read-DiscoveryResult ($output | Select-Object -Last 1)
                }
            } finally { $state.DiscoveryWorker.Dispose(); $state.DiscoveryWorker=$null; $state.DiscoveryHandle=$null }
        }
        if ($state.Busy) { return }
        if (-not $state.DiscoveryPending) { $discoveryTimer.Stop(); return }
        $job=$state.DiscoveryPending; $state.DiscoveryPending=$null
        $state.DiscoveryActiveVersion=$job.Version
        $worker=[PowerShell]::Create()
        $script={
            param($Root,$Job)
            $ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
            Import-Module (Join-Path $Root 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
            $paths=$Job.Paths; $selected=$null; $files=@()
            if ($Job.Startup.Count) {
                foreach ($candidate in $Job.Startup) {
                    try { $found=@(Find-LmsArchives -Path $candidate -Depth $Job.Depth) } catch { continue }
                    if ($found.Count) { $paths=@($candidate); $files=$found; $selected=$candidate; break }
                }
            }
            if (-not $selected) {
                foreach ($path in $paths) {
                    if (-not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($path) -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path)) { $files+=@(Find-LmsArchives -Path $path -Depth $Job.Depth) }
                }
            }
            $rows=@($files | Sort-Object FullName -Unique | ForEach-Object {
                [pscustomobject]@{Name=$_.Name; Folder=$_.Directory.Name; Size=('{0:N1} KB' -f ($_.Length/1KB)); FullPath=$_.FullName}
            })
            [pscustomobject]@{SelectedPath=$selected; Rows=$rows}
        }
        $null=$worker.AddScript($script).AddArgument($PSScriptRoot).AddArgument($job)
        $state.DiscoveryWorker=$worker
        $state.DiscoveryHandle=$worker.BeginInvoke()
    } catch {
        $ui.DiscoveryText.Text='Could not complete ZIP discovery. Check the path and permissions, then Rescan.'
        if ($state.DiscoveryWorker) { $state.DiscoveryWorker.Dispose(); $state.DiscoveryWorker=$null }
        if (-not $state.DiscoveryPending) { $discoveryTimer.Stop() }
    }
})
$ui.RescanButton.Add_Click({ Request-ZipDiscovery -Force })
$ui.AppFolderButton.Add_Click({ Set-SelectedPaths @($state.LaunchFolder); Request-ZipDiscovery -Force })
$ui.ArchiveGrid.Add_SelectionChanged({ $ui.UseDetectedButton.IsEnabled=-not $state.Busy -and $ui.ArchiveGrid.SelectedItems.Count -gt 0 })
$ui.UseDetectedButton.Add_Click({ if (-not $state.Busy) { Set-SelectedPaths @($ui.ArchiveGrid.SelectedItems | ForEach-Object { $_.FullPath }) } })

function Set-SelectedPaths([string[]]$Paths) {
    if (-not $Paths.Count) { return }
    $ui.QueueList.Items.Clear()
    $ui.InputPath.Text=$Paths[0]
    foreach ($entry in @($Paths | Select-Object -Skip 1 -Unique)) { $null=$ui.QueueList.Items.Add($entry) }
    Invalidate-Preview
}
function Update-ReviewFilter {
    if ($null -eq $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource) { return }
    $view=[Windows.Data.CollectionViewSource]::GetDefaultView($ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource)
    if ($ui.UnclassifiedOnly.IsChecked) { $view.Filter=[Predicate[object]]{ param($row); return $row.Action -eq 'Unclassified' -or $row.Reason -like 'Unclassified archive entry*' } }
    else { $view.Filter=$null }
}
$ui.UnclassifiedOnly.Add_Click({ Update-ReviewFilter })
function Get-WorkspaceSnapshot([string]$Name) {
    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Name=$Name; Path=$ui.InputPath.Text; Queue=@($ui.QueueList.Items); Output=$ui.OutputPath.Text
        Task=$ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex; Profile=$ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex; ProfilePath=$ui.ProfilePath.Text
        Group=$ui.GroupChoice.SelectedIndex; Conflict=$ui.ConflictChoice.SelectedIndex
        KeepZip=[bool]$ui.KeepZip.IsChecked; ScanChildren=[bool]$ui.ScanChildren.IsChecked
    }
}
function Restore-Workspace($Saved) {
    foreach ($pair in @(@('Task',3),@('Profile',2),@('Group',2),@('Conflict',1))) {
        if ($null -eq $Saved.($pair[0]) -or [int]$Saved.($pair[0]) -lt 0 -or [int]$Saved.($pair[0]) -gt $pair[1]) { throw 'Saved workspace settings are invalid. Remove it and save a new workspace.' }
    }
    Set-SelectedPaths (@($Saved.Path) + @($Saved.Queue))
    $ui.OutputPath.Text=[string]$Saved.Output
    $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex=[int]$Saved.Task; $ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex=[int]$Saved.Profile
    $ui.ProfilePath.Text=[string]$Saved.ProfilePath; $ui.GroupChoice.SelectedIndex=[int]$Saved.Group
    $ui.ConflictChoice.SelectedIndex=[int]$Saved.Conflict; $ui.KeepZip.IsChecked=[bool]$Saved.KeepZip
    $ui.ScanChildren.IsChecked=[bool]$Saved.ScanChildren; $ui.WorkspaceName.Text=[string]$Saved.Name
    Invalidate-Preview
}
function Edit-SelectedProfile($Seed) {
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
    $profile = switch ($ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex) { 1 { Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles/nust.json' } 2 { $ui.ProfilePath.Text.Trim().Trim('"') } default { '' } }
    $saved=Show-ProfileEditor $window $profile $Seed
    if ($saved) { $ui.ProfilePath.Text=$saved; $ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex=2; Invalidate-Preview; $ui.StatusText.Text='Profile saved and selected. Preview to check the new rules.' }
}
$ui.CancelButton.Add_Click({

})
$ui.QueueAddButton.Add_Click({

$ui.QueueClearButton.Add_Click({ $ui.QueueList.Items.Clear(); Invalidate-Preview })
function Edit-Queue([string]$Action) {
    $index=$ui.QueueList.SelectedIndex
    if ($index -lt 0) { $ui.StatusText.Text='Select an additional queued path first.'; return }
    $path=[string]$ui.QueueList.Items[$index]
    switch ($Action) {
        'Remove' { $ui.QueueList.Items.RemoveAt($index); $ui.QueueList.SelectedIndex=[Math]::Min($index,$ui.QueueList.Items.Count-1) }
        'First' { $old=$ui.InputPath.Text; $ui.QueueList.Items[$index]=$old; $ui.InputPath.Text=$path; $ui.QueueList.SelectedIndex=$index }
        'Up' { if ($index -eq 0) { Edit-Queue 'First'; return }; $ui.QueueList.Items.RemoveAt($index); $ui.QueueList.Items.Insert($index-1,$path); $ui.QueueList.SelectedIndex=$index-1 }
        'Down' { if ($index -ge $ui.QueueList.Items.Count-1) { return }; $ui.QueueList.Items.RemoveAt($index); $ui.QueueList.Items.Insert($index+1,$path); $ui.QueueList.SelectedIndex=$index+1 }
    }
    Invalidate-Preview
}

})

With the default Delete ZIP setting, successfully processed ZIPs are deleted even when different incoming content is skipped. Choose Rename or Keep ZIP before applying if you want to retain it."

$ui.SaveWorkspaceButton.Add_Click({

        Save-Preferences
        $ui.WorkspaceChoice.ItemsSource=@($state.Workspaces)

    } catch { Show-Problem $_.Exception.Message }
})

    }
})

        Edit-SelectedProfile $seed
    } catch { Show-Problem $_.Exception.Message }
})

DesktopFeedback.ps1

Dot-sourced UI helper: progress/elapsed text, taskbar status, ready/working/success/error/cancelled styling and completion actions.

Set-FeedbackStyle

Input: state kind. Applies normal-mode feedback colors for ready/working/success/cancel/error; exits without overrides in Windows high-contrast mode.

Declared at line 1

Resets the title, taskbar, progress and result-folder action when a new plan is required. Does not erase user files or settings.

Declared at line 13

Start-Feedback

Inputs: operation/options. Starts a stopwatch, remembers retention for this run, immediately displays preparation/indeterminate progress and gives Preview/Apply/Undo distinct titles.

Declared at line 22

Update-Feedback

Reads shared progress at a throttled interval. Displays phase, queue/ZIP/entry counts and elapsed time; updates taskbar progress. Cancellation gets a separate stopping state. Percent is phase-local.

Declared at line 36

Complete-Feedback

Input: result. Stops elapsed tracking and selects preview, no-work, cancellation, undo or success feedback. Reports retention accurately and exposes the result-folder action after actual completion.

Declared at line 59

Stop-FeedbackWithError

Stops time tracking, displays partial-work/retained-archive guidance, hides success actions and sets the taskbar error state. It does not pretend failed work was rolled back.

function Set-FeedbackStyle([string]$Kind) {
    if ([Windows.SystemParameters]::HighContrast) { return }
    $colors=switch ($Kind) {
        'Success' { @('#EAF5EE','#14634B') }
        'Error' { @('#FFF0EC','#A33322') }
        'Cancelled' { @('#FFF6DD','#795500') }
        'Working' { @('#EAF2FA','#235B87') }
        default { @('#F5F5F0','#66736D') }
    }
    $ui.FeedbackPanel.Background=[Windows.Media.BrushConverter]::new().ConvertFromString($colors[0])
    $ui.FeedbackPanel.BorderBrush=[Windows.Media.BrushConverter]::new().ConvertFromString($colors[1])
}
function Reset-Feedback {
    $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Ready to preview'
    $ui.WorkItemText.Text=''
    $ui.ResultOpenButton.Visibility='Collapsed'
    $ui.BusyProgress.Visibility='Collapsed'
    $window.Title='LMS Unpacker'
    $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='None'
    Set-FeedbackStyle 'Ready'
}
function Start-Feedback([string]$Operation,[hashtable]$Options) {
    $state.Elapsed=[Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
    $state.LastFeedback=-1000
    $state.KeepZipForRun=[bool]$Options.KeepZip
    $ui.ResultOpenButton.Visibility='Collapsed'
    $ui.WorkItemText.Text='Preparing the selected files...'
    $ui.BusyProgress.IsIndeterminate=$true
    $ui.BusyProgress.Value=0
    $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='Indeterminate'
    $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text=if ($Operation -like '*Preview') { 'Building your preview...' } elseif ($Operation -eq 'UndoApply') { 'Restoring original locations...' } elseif ($Options.ExistingOnly) { 'Organizing your documents...' } else { 'Unpacking your files...' }
    $ui.SummaryText.Text=if ($Operation -like '*Preview') { 'Reading and checking files. Your materials will not be changed.' } else { 'Please keep the app open. Progress is shown for each phase as files are checked and written.' }
    $window.Title=$ui.FeedbackTitle.Text+' - LMS Unpacker'
    Set-FeedbackStyle 'Working'
}
function Update-Feedback {
    if (-not $state.Elapsed -or $state.Elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds-$state.LastFeedback -lt 500) { return }
    $state.LastFeedback=$state.Elapsed.ElapsedMilliseconds
    $p=$state.Control
    $elapsed=$state.Elapsed.Elapsed.ToString('mm\:ss')
    if ($p.CancelRequested) {
        $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Stopping safely...'
        $ui.SummaryText.Text="Finishing the current safe boundary. Unprocessed ZIPs will be kept. Elapsed $elapsed."
        $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='Paused'
        return
    }
    $parts=@($p.Phase)
    if ($state.Operation -notlike 'Undo*') { $parts += "Input $([Math]::Max(1,$p.QueueIndex)) of $($p.QueueTotal)" }
    if ($p.ArchiveTotal -gt 0) { $parts += "ZIP $($p.ArchiveIndex) of $($p.ArchiveTotal)" }
    if ($p.Total -gt 0) {
        $parts += "$($p.Completed) of $($p.Total) entries in this phase"
        $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='Normal'
        $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressValue=[Math]::Min(1.0,[double]$p.Completed / $p.Total)
    } else { $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='Indeterminate' }
    $parts += "Elapsed $elapsed"
    $ui.SummaryText.Text=$parts -join ' | '
    $ui.WorkItemText.Text=[string]$p.Current
}
function Complete-Feedback($Result) {
    $state.Elapsed.Stop()
    $duration=if ($state.Elapsed.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -lt 60) { '{0:0.0} seconds' -f $state.Elapsed.Elapsed.TotalSeconds } else { $state.Elapsed.Elapsed.ToString('hh\:mm\:ss') }
    $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='None'
    $ui.WorkItemText.Text='Elapsed '+$duration
    $ui.ResultOpenButton.Visibility='Collapsed'
    if ($Result.PSObject.Properties['Cancelled'] -and $Result.Cancelled) {
        if ($Result.Preview) {
            $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Preview stopped - no files changed'
            $ui.SummaryText.Text='The partial plan has been discarded. Preview again when you are ready.'
        } else {
            $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Stopped safely - some work may be complete'
            $ui.SummaryText.Text="Completed before stopping: $($Result.Archives) ZIPs, $($Result.Written) extracted, $($Result.Skipped) skipped, $($Result.Moved) organized. Unprocessed ZIPs were kept. Preview again to continue."
        }
        Set-FeedbackStyle 'Cancelled'
    } elseif ($Result.Preview) {
        $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text=if ($ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled) { 'Preview ready - no files changed' } else { 'Nothing to apply' }
        Set-FeedbackStyle 'Ready'
    } elseif ($state.Operation -eq 'UndoApply') {
        $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Done - original locations restored'
        Set-FeedbackStyle 'Success'
    } else {
        $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Done - your files are ready'
        $zipStatus=if ($state.KeepZipForRun) { 'Source ZIPs were kept.' } elseif ($Result.Archives -gt 0) { 'Successfully processed ZIPs were permanently deleted, including skipped incoming files.' } else { 'No ZIPs were processed.' }
        $ui.SummaryText.Text="ZIPs processed: $($Result.Archives) | Extracted: $($Result.Written) | Skipped: $($Result.Skipped) | Organized: $($Result.Moved) | Unclassified: $($Result.Unclassified). $zipStatus"
        $ui.ResultOpenButton.Content=if ($state.Control.QueueTotal -gt 1) { '_Open first result folder' } else { '_Open result folder' }
        $ui.ResultOpenButton.Visibility='Visible'
        Set-FeedbackStyle 'Success'
    }
    $window.Title=$ui.FeedbackTitle.Text+' - LMS Unpacker'
}
function Stop-FeedbackWithError {
    if ($state.Elapsed) { $state.Elapsed.Stop() }
    $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text='Needs attention - processing stopped'
    $ui.SummaryText.Text='Some files may already have been processed. Affected ZIPs were kept. See Activity and errors before trying again.'
    $ui.WorkItemText.Text=''
    $ui.ResultOpenButton.Visibility='Collapsed'
    $window.Title='Needs attention - LMS Unpacker'
    $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState='Error'
    $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressValue=1
    Set-FeedbackStyle 'Error'
}
$ui.ResultOpenButton.Add_Click({ $ui.OpenFolderButton.RaiseEvent([Windows.RoutedEventArgs]::new([Windows.Controls.Button]::ClickEvent)) })

DesktopIntegration.psm1

Exports per-user EXE copying and Start shortcut creation. Releases COM handles; shortcut installation is optional.

Add-LmsStartShortcut

Inputs: EXE and optional install/shortcut paths. Copies to a stable per-user Programs location with temporary publication, creates a WScript.Shell shortcut to that copy and releases COM objects. Returns installed target; tests override locations.

Declared at line 2
Read the full source (35 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
function Add-LmsStartShortcut {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Executable,
        [string]$InstallFolder = (Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('LocalApplicationData')) 'Programs\LMS Unpacker'),
        [string]$ShortcutPath = (Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('Programs')) 'LMS Unpacker.lnk')
    )
    $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
    $source = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $Executable).FullName
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($InstallFolder)
    $target = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $InstallFolder 'LMS-Unpacker.exe'))
    if ($source -ne $target) {
        $temporary = Join-Path $InstallFolder ('update-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '.tmp')
        try {
            [IO.File]::Copy($source,$temporary,$false)
            if ([IO.File]::Exists($target)) { [IO.File]::Replace($temporary,$target,[NullString]::Value) }
            else { [IO.File]::Move($temporary,$target) }
        } finally { if ([IO.File]::Exists($temporary)) { [IO.File]::Delete($temporary) } }
    }
    $shell = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
    try {
        $link = $shell.CreateShortcut($ShortcutPath)
        try {
            $link.TargetPath = $target
            $link.WorkingDirectory = [Environment]::GetFolderPath('UserProfile')
            $link.Description = 'Unpack ZIPs and organize your courses'
            $link.IconLocation = $target + ',0'
            $link.Save()
        } finally { $null = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($link) }
    } finally { $null = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($shell) }
    return $target
}
Export-ModuleMember -Function Add-LmsStartShortcut

DesktopPaths.psm1

Pure path-choice helpers for actual EXE versus working directory, remembered picker fallback and explicit/startup input priority.

Get-LmsLaunchFolder

Inputs: actual EXE path and working directory. Chooses an existing EXE parent, then working directory, then UserProfile. Keeps the embedded temporary runtime out of the user-facing launch location.

Declared at line 2

Get-LmsPickerFolder

Inputs: remembered location and launch folder. Returns the first existing directory, then UserProfile fallback. Used independently for folder and ZIP dialogs.

Declared at line 7

Get-LmsStartupSelection

Inputs: explicit path, launch folder and remembered selection. Returns initial Path and ordered unique Candidates. Explicit input is the only candidate when supplied; otherwise launch ZIP discovery can take priority over memory.

Declared at line 13
Read the full source (20 lines)
# Pure path decisions shared by desktop startup, dialogs and integration checks.
function Get-LmsLaunchFolder([string]$Executable,[string]$WorkingDirectory) {
    if ($Executable -and [IO.File]::Exists($Executable)) { return [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName([IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Executable)) }
    if ([IO.Directory]::Exists($WorkingDirectory)) { return [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($WorkingDirectory) }
    return [Environment]::GetFolderPath('UserProfile')
}
function Get-LmsPickerFolder([string]$Remembered,[string]$LaunchFolder) {
    foreach ($candidate in @($Remembered,$LaunchFolder)) {
        if ([IO.Directory]::Exists($candidate)) { return [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($candidate) }
    }
    return [Environment]::GetFolderPath('UserProfile')
}
function Get-LmsStartupSelection([string]$ExplicitPath,[string]$LaunchFolder,[string]$Remembered) {
    if ($ExplicitPath) { return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$ExplicitPath; Candidates=@($ExplicitPath)} }
    $lastValid=[IO.Directory]::Exists($Remembered) -or [IO.File]::Exists($Remembered)
    $initial=if ($lastValid) { $Remembered } else { $LaunchFolder }
    return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$initial; Candidates=@(@($LaunchFolder,$initial) | Select-Object -Unique)}
}
Export-ModuleMember -Function Get-LmsLaunchFolder,Get-LmsPickerFolder,Get-LmsStartupSelection

HistoryDialog.ps1

Lists local undo journals and shows a selection dialog. Listing is bounded; the engine remains responsible for full restore validation.

Test-HistoryPlainPath

Input: candidate history path. Returns false if it or an existing ancestor is a link/reparse point; supports safe read-only journal indexing.

Declared at line 2

Get-OrganizationHistory

Input: selected paths/queue. Deduplicates history locations, lists at most 500 recent records per location, rejects oversized/linked/malformed records and returns status rows. Availability is provisional until engine undo validation.

Declared at line 12

Show-HistoryDialog

Inputs: owner, paths and optional test Inspect callback. Builds the real WPF record picker, supports selecting another root and returns the chosen report path. Test hook inspects the dialog without displaying it.

Declared at line 39
Read the full source (69 lines)
# History is an index of local organization journals. Undo validates every path and hash again.
function Test-HistoryPlainPath([string]$Path) {
    $part=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
    while ($part) {
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $part) {
            if ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $part -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) { return $false }
        }
        $part=[IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($part)
    }
    return $true
}
function Get-OrganizationHistory([string[]]$Paths) {
    $seen=@{}
    foreach ($path in $Paths) {
        if (-not $path.Trim()) { continue }
        try {
            $root=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($path.Trim().Trim('"'))
            if (-not [IO.Directory]::Exists($root)) { $root=[IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($root) }
            $folder=Join-Path $root '.lms-unpacker/history'
            if ($seen.ContainsKey($folder) -or -not (Test-HistoryPlainPath $folder) -or -not [IO.Directory]::Exists($folder)) { continue }
            $seen[$folder]=$true
            foreach ($file in @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $folder -Filter '*.json' -File | Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 500)) {
                $status='Unreadable record'; $count=0; $canPreview=$false
                try {
                    if ($file.Length -gt 10MB -or -not (Test-HistoryPlainPath $file.FullName)) { throw 'Unsafe or oversized record' }
                    $journal=Get-Content -LiteralPath $file.FullName -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
                    if ($journal.Version -ne 1 -or [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($journal.Root) -ne $root -or -not $journal.PSObject.Properties['Moves']) { throw 'Invalid record' }
                    $moves=@($journal.Moves)
                    if (@($moves | Where-Object { $_.State -notin @('Pending','Moved','Restoring','Undone') }).Count) { throw 'Unknown state' }
                    $count=@($moves | Where-Object { $_.State -ne 'Undone' }).Count
                    $status=if ($count) { 'Available for validation' } else { 'Already undone / empty' }
                    $canPreview=$count -gt 0
                } catch { }
                [pscustomobject]@{Updated=$file.LastWriteTime.ToString('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm'); Root=$root; Remaining=$count; Status=$status; Path=$file.FullName; CanPreview=$canPreview}
            }
        } catch { }
    }
}
function Show-HistoryDialog($Owner,[string[]]$Paths,[scriptblock]$Inspect) {
    [xml]$markup=@'
<Window xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation" xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml" Title="Organization history" Width="850" Height="480" MinWidth="640" MinHeight="360" WindowStartupLocation="CenterOwner" FontFamily="Segoe UI" FontSize="14" Background="{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.WindowBrushKey}}" Foreground="{DynamicResource {x:Static SystemColors.WindowTextBrushKey}}">
 <Grid Margin="18"><Grid.RowDefinitions><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/><RowDefinition Height="*"/><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/></Grid.RowDefinitions>
 <TextBlock Text="Undo moves of loose documents. ZIP extraction and ZIP deletion cannot be undone here. Select a record, then preview; files and hashes are checked before any restore." TextWrapping="Wrap" Margin="0,0,0,12"/>
 <DataGrid Grid.Row="1" x:Name="Records" AutoGenerateColumns="False" IsReadOnly="True" CanUserAddRows="False" SelectionMode="Single" AutomationProperties.Name="Organization history records"><DataGrid.Columns><DataGridTextColumn Header="Updated" Binding="{Binding Updated}" Width="145"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Folder" Binding="{Binding Root}" Width="*"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Remaining" Binding="{Binding Remaining}" Width="85"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Status" Binding="{Binding Status}" Width="190"/></DataGrid.Columns></DataGrid>
 <TextBlock Grid.Row="2" x:Name="Hint" TextWrapping="Wrap" Margin="0,10" AutomationProperties.LiveSetting="Polite"/>
 <WrapPanel Grid.Row="3"><Button x:Name="Preview" Content="_Preview undo" Padding="14,9" Margin="0,0,8,0" IsEnabled="False"/><Button x:Name="Folder" Content="Choose another _folder..." Padding="14,9" Margin="0,0,8,0"/><Button x:Name="Browse" Content="Browse _record..." Padding="14,9" Margin="0,0,8,0"/><Button Content="_Cancel" IsCancel="True" Padding="14,9"/></WrapPanel>
 </Grid>
</Window>
'@
    $reader=[Xml.XmlNodeReader]::new($markup)
    try { $dialog=[Windows.Markup.XamlReader]::Load($reader) } finally { $reader.Close() }
    $records=$dialog.FindName('Records'); $hint=$dialog.FindName('Hint'); $preview=$dialog.FindName('Preview')
    $records.ItemsSource=@(Get-OrganizationHistory $Paths)
    $hint.Text='Showing up to 500 recent records per selected folder. If no records appear, choose the folder you originally organized.'
    $records.Add_SelectionChanged({ $preview.IsEnabled=$null -ne $records.SelectedItem -and $records.SelectedItem.CanPreview; if ($records.SelectedItem) { $hint.Text=$records.SelectedItem.Path } })
    $preview.Add_Click({ if ($records.SelectedItem.CanPreview) { $dialog.Tag=$records.SelectedItem.Path; $dialog.DialogResult=$true } })
    $dialog.FindName('Folder').Add_Click({
        $picker=[Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog]::new(); $picker.Description='Choose the folder originally organized'; $picker.ShowNewFolderButton=$false
        try { if ($picker.ShowDialog() -eq 'OK') { $records.ItemsSource=@(Get-OrganizationHistory @($picker.SelectedPath)); $hint.Text='Select a record to preview undo. Only this folder is searched.' } } finally { $picker.Dispose() }
    })
    $dialog.FindName('Browse').Add_Click({
        $picker=[Microsoft.Win32.OpenFileDialog]::new(); $picker.Title='Choose an original organization record'; $picker.Filter='Undo records (*.json)|*.json'
        if ($picker.ShowDialog($dialog)) { $dialog.Tag=$picker.FileName; $dialog.DialogResult=$true }
    })
    if ($Inspect) { & $Inspect $dialog $records; return }
    $dialog.Owner=$Owner
    if ($dialog.ShowDialog()) { return [string]$dialog.Tag }
}

LmsUnpacker.psm1

Shared engine: validates paths/profiles, scans, classifies, plans conflicts, verifies/stages/publishes archives, organizes loose documents and validates undo.

Assert-PlainPath

Input: a filesystem path. Walks it and its ancestors, allowing missing components but rejecting reparse points and file-blocked parent directories. Throws before unsafe reads/writes; returns no business data.

Declared at line 8

Get-SafeRelativePath

Input: archive/rule path text. Normalizes separators and rejects rooted, empty, dot, traversal, reserved-name and unsupported-character components. Returns a platform-separated relative path or throws.

Declared at line 24

Get-ContainedPath

Inputs: trusted root and untrusted relative name. Combines validated components, normalizes the full path, enforces root-with-separator containment and checks links. Returns the allowed absolute destination.

Declared at line 36

Read-LmsRules

Input: optional JSON path. Returns an empty general profile or validates Version/Courses/Rules, compiles case-insensitive wildcard matchers, sorts course codes longest-first and creates a per-course rule cache. Invalid profiles stop the operation.

Declared at line 45

Get-LmsCourse

Inputs: ZIP stem or folder name and course mappings. Finds an exact folder match or bounded escaped course-code prefix. Returns the matching course or null; never guesses from a loose substring.

Declared at line 78

Get-LmsCategory

Input: filename. Recognizes ordered category keywords/numbers after separator normalization. Protects submission/evidence names and returns null when uncertain. No document-content reading occurs.

Declared at line 85

Get-FileDigest

Input: existing path. Returns the SHA-256 string using LiteralPath hashing. Used for collision comparison and journal validation; errors propagate.

Declared at line 99

Get-LmsClassification

Inputs: filename, course, profile, grouping and LMS context. Tries ordered matching rules, then filename categories/context weeks; transforms lecture grouping and returns Folder/Topic/Reason. Missing weeks/topics stay explicitly unknown.

Declared at line 101

Get-LmsTarget

Inputs: proposed target, incoming hash, conflict policy and mutable reservation map. Compares existing or planned content; returns Path/Action/Comparison. Reserves writes, skips identical data and searches numbered names under Rename. Throws on blocking directories.

Declared at line 142

Copy-LmsEntry

Inputs: ZipArchiveEntry, optional staging path and expected CRC. Opens streams, delegates streaming integrity/hash work to CopyChecked and disposes both. Preview omits the output stream; Apply uses CreateNew staging files.

Declared at line 173

Replace-LmsFile

Inputs: staged source and destination. Rechecks plain paths and attempts atomic File.Replace. Retries IOException at bounded exponential delays while the source remains; all other failures propagate.

Declared at line 185

Install-LmsFile

Inputs: staged source, final target and replace flag. Creates the parent, copies into a unique sibling temporary file, then Move/Replace publishes it. Finally removes any owned leftover temporary file. Atomic per file, not per archive.

Declared at line 201

Invoke-LmsArchive

Inputs: ZIP FileInfo, normalized options, profile and archive-local reservation map. Routes course, validates metadata/types/limits, hashes and stages all entries, checks internal path conflicts, publishes planned files and optionally deletes the source. Returns counts/details; finally disposes archive and removes owned staging.

Declared at line 215

Save-LmsJournal

Inputs: journal object and report path. Serializes JSON to a sibling .tmp and moves/replaces the report. Durable intermediate states allow recovery after interrupted moves; it is not a backup of file bytes.

Declared at line 335

Invoke-LmsOrganization

Inputs: scanned files, root, options, profile and reservation map. Handles only eligible loose document extensions, classifies and plans, leaves unknown/colliding sources intact, hashes actual moves and persists Pending/Moved records around File.Move. Returns moved/unclassified counts, journal and details.

Declared at line 343

Get-LmsScanFiles

Inputs: filesystem item and depth 0/1. Returns direct files plus allowed immediate child-folder files. Excludes protected/hidden child folders and links. An explicitly selected file is returned directly. Shared by discovery and processing.

Declared at line 404

Find-LmsArchives

Exported read-only discovery API. Inputs: path and validated depth. Resolves a filesystem item, calls the shared scan and returns ZIP FileInfo objects sorted by full path. Does not open archive contents.

Declared at line 419

Invoke-LmsUnpacker

Exported orchestration API. Validates option combinations/root/profile/output, scans, optionally organizes loose files, then processes archives with per-archive reservation copies. Aggregates structured counts/details and logs. Archive errors retain ZIPs; loose-organization errors stop ZIP processing.

Declared at line 427

Undo-LmsOrganization

Exported undo API. Reads a Version 1 journal at its original history location, validates contained paths and hashes and builds reverse moves before mutation. Preview prints only; Apply persists Restoring/Undone states. Recognizes recoverable interrupted states and refuses occupied/modified files.

Declared at line 511

Test-LmsProfile

Exported validator. Reads rules through the same private validation path used by extraction; returns true on success or throws. Editors/importers use it before publishing a profile.
