#requires -Version 5.1
Set-StrictMode -Version Latest
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
if (-not ('LmsUnpacker.ZipIntegrity' -as [type])) { Add-Type -Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'ZipIntegrity.cs') }

function Assert-PlainPath([string]$Path) {
    $cursor = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
    $target = $cursor
    while ($cursor) {
        try {
            $attributes = [IO.File]::GetAttributes($cursor)
            if ($attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) { throw "Links/junctions are not supported: $cursor" }
            if ($cursor -ne $target -and -not ($attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::Directory)) { throw "A file blocks a folder: $cursor" }
        } catch [IO.FileNotFoundException] { }
        catch [IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] { }
        $parent = [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($cursor)
        if ($parent -eq $cursor) { break }
        $cursor = $parent
    }
}

function Get-SafeRelativePath([string]$Name) {
    $normalized = $Name.Replace('\','/').TrimEnd('/')
    if (-not $normalized -or $normalized.StartsWith('/')) { throw "Unsafe archive/rule path: $Name" }
    foreach ($part in $normalized.Split('/')) {
        if (-not $part -or $part -in @('.','..') -or $part -match '[<>:"|?*\x00-\x1f]' -or
            $part -match '[. ]$' -or $part -match '^(?i:CON|PRN|AUX|NUL|COM[1-9]|LPT[1-9])(?:\.|$)') {
            throw "Unsupported archive/rule path: $Name"
        }
    }
    return $normalized.Replace('/',[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar)
}

function Get-ContainedPath([string]$Root, [string]$Relative) {
    $safe = Get-SafeRelativePath $Relative
    $base = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Root).TrimEnd('\','/') + [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
    $target = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $base $safe))
    if (-not $target.StartsWith($base,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw "Path escapes destination: $Relative" }
    Assert-PlainPath $target
    return $target
}

function Read-LmsRules([string]$Path) {
    if (-not $Path) { return [pscustomobject]@{Version=1; Courses=@(); Rules=@(); RuleCache=@{}} }
    $config = Get-Content -LiteralPath $Path -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    if ($config.Version -ne 1) { throw 'Rules Version must be 1.' }
    if (-not $config.PSObject.Properties['Courses']) { $config | Add-Member NoteProperty Courses @() }
    $codes = @{}
    $folders = @{}
    foreach ($course in $config.Courses) {
        if ($course.Code -notmatch '^[A-Za-z0-9]+(?:-[A-Za-z0-9]+)*$') { throw 'Course codes may contain letters, numbers and hyphens.' }
        $safe = Get-SafeRelativePath $course.Folder
        if ($safe -match '[/\\]' -or $safe.StartsWith('.')) { throw 'Course Folder must be a single, visible folder name.' }
        if ($codes.ContainsKey($course.Code) -or $folders.ContainsKey($course.Folder)) { throw 'Course codes and folders must be unique.' }
        $codes[$course.Code] = $true
        $folders[$course.Folder] = $true
    }
    foreach ($rule in $config.Rules) {
        if (-not $rule.Course -or -not $rule.Pattern -or -not $rule.Folder) { throw 'Each rule needs Course, Pattern, and Folder.' }
        $null = Get-SafeRelativePath $rule.Folder
        if ($rule.PSObject.Properties['Topic'] -and $rule.Topic) {
            $topic = Get-SafeRelativePath $rule.Topic
            if ($topic -match '[/\\]') { throw 'A rule Topic must be a single folder name.' }
        }
        $rule | Add-Member NoteProperty Matcher ([Management.Automation.WildcardPattern]::new($rule.Pattern, 'IgnoreCase')) -Force
        $rule | Add-Member NoteProperty CourseMatcher ([Management.Automation.WildcardPattern]::new($rule.Course, 'IgnoreCase')) -Force
        # Support ordinary LMS space/underscore/hyphen variants without changing wildcard character classes.
        $normalized = if ($rule.Pattern -notmatch '\[') { [Management.Automation.WildcardPattern]::new(($rule.Pattern -replace '[_\s-]+',' '), 'IgnoreCase') } else { $null }
        $rule | Add-Member NoteProperty NormalizedMatcher $normalized -Force
    }
    $config.Courses = @($config.Courses | Sort-Object { $_.Code.Length } -Descending)
    $config | Add-Member NoteProperty RuleCache @{} -Force
    return $config
}

function Get-LmsCourse([string]$Text, [object[]]$Courses) {
    foreach ($course in $Courses) {
        if ($Text -eq $course.Folder -or $Text -match ('^(?i:' + [regex]::Escape($course.Code) + ')(?:[^a-zA-Z0-9]|$)')) { return $course }
    }
    return $null
}

function Get-LmsCategory([string]$Name) {
    $stem = [IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($Name).Replace('_',' ').Replace('-',' ')
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(submission|student copy|evidence)\b') { return $null }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(course outline|syllab(us|i)|course schedule)\b') { return 'Course Info' }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(assignment|homework)\b') { return 'Assignments' }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(quiz|quizzes|exam|midterm|past paper)\b') { return 'Quizzes and Exams' }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(book|textbook|edition|\d+(st|nd|rd|th) ed|\d+ed)\b') { return 'Books' }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\blab(?:oratory)?\s*0*(\d+)\b') { return ('Labs/Lab {0:D2}' -f [int]$Matches[1]) }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(practice|sample questions|problem sheet|exercises)\b') { return 'Practice' }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\bweek\s*0*(\d+)\b') { return ('Lectures/Week {0:D2}' -f [int]$Matches[1]) }
    if ($stem -match '(?i)\b(lecture|lec)\s*\d*') { return 'Lectures' }
    return $null
}

function Get-FileDigest([string]$Path) { return (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $Path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash }

function Get-LmsClassification([string]$Name, [string]$Course, $Profile, [string]$GroupBy, [string]$Context) {
    if (-not $Profile.RuleCache.ContainsKey($Course)) {
        $Profile.RuleCache[$Course] = @($Profile.Rules | Where-Object { $_.CourseMatcher.IsMatch($Course) })
    }
    $match = $null
    $normalizedName = $Name -replace '[_\s-]+',' '
    foreach ($rule in $Profile.RuleCache[$Course]) {
        if ($rule.Matcher.IsMatch($Name) -or ($rule.NormalizedMatcher -and $rule.NormalizedMatcher.IsMatch($normalizedName))) { $match = $rule; break }
    }
    $category = $null
    $topic = $null
    $reason = 'No matching filename or syllabus rule; unchanged.'
    if ($match) {
        $category = $match.Folder
        $reason = 'Profile rule: ' + $match.Pattern
        if ($match.PSObject.Properties['Reason'] -and $match.Reason) { $reason += ' - ' + $match.Reason }
        if ($match.PSObject.Properties['Topic']) { $topic = $match.Topic }
    } else {
        $category = Get-LmsCategory $Name
        if ($category) { $reason = 'Recognized from the filename.' }
    }
    # LMS section names can provide a week when the filename does not.
    $protected = -not $category -and $Name -match '(?i)(submission|student[ _-]+copy|evidence)'
    if (-not $protected -and ($null -eq $category -or $category -eq 'Lectures') -and
        $Context -match '(?i)(?:^|[/\\ ])week[ _-]*0*(\d{1,2})(?:\b|_)') {
        $category = 'Lectures/Week {0:D2}' -f [int]$Matches[1]
        $reason = 'Explicit week label in the filename or LMS folder.'
    }
    if ($category -match '^Lectures(?:[/\\]|$)') {
        if ($GroupBy -eq 'Category') { $category = 'Lectures' }
        elseif ($GroupBy -eq 'Topic') {
            if ($topic) { $category = 'Lectures/' + $topic }
            else { $category = 'Lectures/Unsorted topics'; $reason += ' No topic rule is available.' }
        } elseif ($category -eq 'Lectures') {
            $category = 'Lectures/Unscheduled'
            $reason += ' No reliable week is available.'
        }
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{Folder=$category; Topic=$topic; Reason=$reason}
}

function Get-LmsTarget([string]$Target, [string]$Hash, [string]$Conflict, [hashtable]$Planned) {
    $candidate = $Target
    $number = 0
    $comparison = ''
    while ($true) {
        Assert-PlainPath $candidate
        $exists = Test-Path -LiteralPath $candidate
        if ($exists -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $candidate -PathType Leaf)) { throw "A folder blocks a file: $candidate" }
        if (-not $exists -and -not $Planned.ContainsKey($candidate)) {
            $Planned[$candidate] = $Hash
            return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$candidate; Action='Write'; Comparison=$comparison}
        }
        $existingHash = if ($Planned.ContainsKey($candidate)) { $Planned[$candidate] } else { Get-FileDigest $candidate }
        if ($existingHash.StartsWith('preview-source:')) {
            $existingHash = Get-FileDigest $existingHash.Substring(15)
            $Planned[$candidate] = $existingHash
        }
        $origin = if ($Planned.ContainsKey($candidate)) { 'earlier planned output' } else { 'existing file' }
        $match = if ($existingHash -eq $Hash) { 'Identical content (SHA-256)' } else { 'Different content (SHA-256)' }
        $comparison = "$match versus $origin at $candidate. Incoming SHA-256: $Hash. Compared SHA-256: $existingHash."
        if ($existingHash -eq $Hash) { return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$candidate; Action='Duplicate'; Comparison=$comparison} }
        if ($Conflict -eq 'Skip') { return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$candidate; Action='Skip'; Comparison=$comparison} }
        if ($Conflict -eq 'Overwrite') {
            $Planned[$candidate] = $Hash
            return [pscustomobject]@{Path=$candidate; Action='Overwrite'; Comparison=$comparison}
        }
        $number++
        $candidate = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($Target)) ('{0}_{1}{2}' -f [IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($Target), $number, [IO.Path]::GetExtension($Target))
    }
}

function Copy-LmsEntry($Entry, [string]$OutputPath, [uint32]$Crc) {
    $inputStream = $Entry.Open()
    $outputStream = $null
    try {
        if ($OutputPath) { $outputStream = [IO.File]::Open($OutputPath, 'CreateNew', 'Write', 'None') }
        return [LmsUnpacker.ZipIntegrity]::CopyChecked($inputStream,$outputStream,$Entry.Length,$Crc)
    } finally {
        $inputStream.Dispose()
        if ($outputStream) { $outputStream.Dispose() }
    }
}

function Replace-LmsFile([string]$Source, [string]$Target) {
    # Indexers/virus scanners can briefly hold a destination without delete sharing.
    # Keep the staged source intact and retry only bounded I/O failures.
    for ($attempt = 0; $attempt -le 4; $attempt++) {
        Assert-PlainPath $Source
        Assert-PlainPath $Target
        try {
            [IO.File]::Replace($Source,$Target,[NullString]::Value)
            return
        } catch [IO.IOException] {
            if ($attempt -eq 4 -or -not [IO.File]::Exists($Source)) { throw }
            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds (100 * [math]::Pow(2,$attempt))
        }
    }
}

function Install-LmsFile([string]$Source, [string]$Target, [bool]$Replace) {
    Assert-PlainPath $Target
    $parent = [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($Target)
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($parent)
    $temporary = Join-Path $parent ('.lms-write-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
    try {
        [IO.File]::Copy($Source,$temporary,$false)
        if ($Replace -and [IO.File]::Exists($Target)) { Replace-LmsFile $temporary $Target }
        else { [IO.File]::Move($temporary,$Target) }
    } finally {
        if ([IO.File]::Exists($temporary)) { [IO.File]::Delete($temporary) }
    }
}

function Invoke-LmsArchive($Zip, $Options, $Profile, [hashtable]$Planned) {
    $archive = $null
    $scratch = $null
    $items = New-Object 'Collections.Generic.List[object]'
    try {
        $courseRoot = $Zip.DirectoryName
        if ($Options.OutputPath) { $courseRoot = $Options.OutputPath }
        $courseName = $Zip.Directory.Name
        if ($Options.Mode -eq 'Organize') {
            $course = Get-LmsCourse $Zip.BaseName $Profile.Courses
            $parentCourse = Get-LmsCourse $Zip.Directory.Name $Profile.Courses
            if ($course) {
                if ($parentCourse -and $parentCourse.Code -ne $course.Code) { throw 'ZIP course code conflicts with its containing course folder.' }
                if ($Options.OutputPath) { $courseRoot = Get-ContainedPath $Options.OutputPath $course.Folder }
                elseif (-not $parentCourse) { $courseRoot = Get-ContainedPath $Options.Root $course.Folder }
                $courseName = $course.Code
            } elseif ($parentCourse) {
                $courseName = $parentCourse.Code
                if ($Options.OutputPath) { $courseRoot = Get-ContainedPath $Options.OutputPath $parentCourse.Folder }
            }
        }
        Assert-PlainPath $Zip.FullName
        $checksums = [LmsUnpacker.ZipIntegrity]::ReadChecksums($Zip.FullName,$Options.MaxEntries)
        $archive = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($Zip.FullName)
        if ($checksums.Length -ne $archive.Entries.Count) { throw 'Archive entry count does not match its directory.' }
        if ($archive.Entries.Count -gt $Options.MaxEntries) { throw "Archive exceeds $($Options.MaxEntries) entries." }
        [long]$total = 0
        foreach ($entry in $archive.Entries) {
            $relative = Get-SafeRelativePath $entry.FullName
            $unixType = (($entry.ExternalAttributes -shr 16) -band 0xF000)
            if ($unixType -ne 0 -and $unixType -ne 0x8000 -and $unixType -ne 0x4000) { throw "Unsupported special entry: $relative" }
            $total += $entry.Length
            if ($total -gt $Options.MaxExpandedBytes) { throw 'Archive exceeds the expanded size limit.' }
        }
        if (-not $Options.Preview) {
            $scratch = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('lms-unpack-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
            $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($scratch)
        }
        $entryIndex = -1
        foreach ($entry in $archive.Entries) {
            $entryIndex++
            if ($Options.Control.CancelRequested) { throw [OperationCanceledException]::new('Cancelled before archive publication; ZIP retained.') }
            $Options.Control.Current = $entry.FullName
            $Options.Control.Completed = $entryIndex
            $Options.Control.Total = $archive.Entries.Count
            $Options.Control.Phase = 'Reading archive'
            $relative = Get-SafeRelativePath $entry.FullName
            $isDirectory = $entry.FullName.EndsWith('/') -or $entry.FullName.EndsWith('\')
            if ($isDirectory -and ($entry.Length -ne 0 -or $checksums[$entryIndex] -ne 0)) { throw 'Directory entry contains unexpected data.' }
            $root = $courseRoot
            $reason = 'Original archive structure.'
            if ($Options.Mode -eq 'Extract') {
                $root = Get-ContainedPath $root $Zip.BaseName
                $target = Get-ContainedPath $root $relative
            } else {
                if ($isDirectory) { continue }
                $name = [IO.Path]::GetFileName($relative)
                $reason = 'Flattened archive entry.'
                if ($Options.Mode -eq 'Organize') {
                    $classification = Get-LmsClassification $name $courseName $Profile $Options.GroupBy $relative
                    $reason = $classification.Reason
                    if ($classification.Folder) { $root = Get-ContainedPath $root $classification.Folder }
                    else { $reason = 'Unclassified archive entry; extracted beside the ZIP.' }
                }
                $target = Get-ContainedPath $root $name
            }
            if ($target -eq $Zip.FullName -or $Options.Sources.ContainsKey($target)) { throw "Output would replace an input archive: $target" }
            if ($isDirectory) {
                if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target -PathType Leaf) { throw "A file blocks a folder: $target" }
                $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{Path=$target; Action='Directory'; Stage=$null; Source=$relative; Reason=$reason})
                continue
            }
            $stage = if ($scratch) { Join-Path $scratch ($items.Count.ToString() + '.data') } else { $null }
            $hash = Copy-LmsEntry $entry $stage $checksums[$entryIndex]
            $decision = Get-LmsTarget $target $hash $Options.Conflict $Planned
            $items.Add([pscustomobject]@{Path=$decision.Path; Action=$decision.Action; Stage=$stage; Source=$relative; Reason=$reason; Course=$courseName; Comparison=$decision.Comparison; Bytes=$entry.Length; Archive=$Zip.FullName})
        }
        $filePaths = @{}
        foreach ($item in $items) { if ($item.Action -ne 'Directory') { $filePaths[$item.Path] = $true } }
        foreach ($item in $items) {
            $parent = if ($item.Action -eq 'Directory') { $item.Path } else { [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($item.Path) }
            while ($parent) {
                if ($filePaths.ContainsKey($parent) -or (Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent -PathType Leaf)) { throw "A file blocks a folder: $parent" }
                $parent = [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($parent)
            }
        }
        if ($Options.Control.CancelRequested) { throw [OperationCanceledException]::new('Cancelled before archive publication; ZIP retained.') }
        $Options.Control.Phase = if ($Options.Preview) { 'Completing archive preview' } else { 'Writing verified files' }
        $Options.Control.Completed=0
        $Options.Control.Total=$items.Count
        foreach ($item in $items) {
            $Options.Control.Current=$item.Source
            Write-Host "  [$($item.Action)] $($item.Path)"
            if ($item.Action -ne 'Directory' -and $item.Comparison) { Write-Host "    $($item.Comparison)" }
            if ($Options.Mode -eq 'Organize' -and $item.Action -ne 'Directory') { Write-Host "    Why: $($item.Reason)" }
            if (-not $Options.Preview) {
                if ($item.Action -eq 'Directory') { $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($item.Path) }
                elseif ($item.Action -in @('Write','Overwrite')) { Install-LmsFile $item.Stage $item.Path ($item.Action -eq 'Overwrite') }
            }
            $Options.Control.Completed++
        }
        $archive.Dispose()
        $archive = $null
        if (-not $Options.KeepZip) {
            if (-not $Options.Preview) { [IO.File]::Delete($Zip.FullName) }
            Write-Host "  [Delete ZIP] $($Zip.FullName)"
        } else { Write-Host "  [Keep ZIP] $($Zip.FullName)" }
        $Options.Control.Completed = $Options.Control.Total
        return [pscustomobject]@{
            Written=@($items | Where-Object { $_.Action -in @('Write','Overwrite') }).Count
            Skipped=@($items | Where-Object { $_.Action -in @('Skip','Duplicate') }).Count
            Unclassified=@($items | Where-Object { $_.Reason -like 'Unclassified archive entry*' }).Count
            Details=@($items | Select-Object Source, @{Name='Target';Expression={$_.Path}}, Action, Reason, Course, Comparison, Bytes, Archive)
        }
    } finally {
        if ($archive) { $archive.Dispose() }
        if ($scratch -and [IO.Directory]::Exists($scratch)) { [IO.Directory]::Delete($scratch,$true) }
    }
}

function Save-LmsJournal($Journal, [string]$Path) {
    $temporary = $Path + '.tmp'
    $json = $Journal | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($temporary,$json,[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    if ([IO.File]::Exists($Path)) { Replace-LmsFile $temporary $Path }
    else { [IO.File]::Move($temporary,$Path) }
}

function Invoke-LmsOrganization($Files, [string]$Root, $Options, $Profile, [hashtable]$Planned) {
    $journal = [pscustomobject]@{Version=1; Root=$Root; Moves=@()}
    $journalPath = $null
    $moved = 0
    $details = New-Object 'Collections.Generic.List[object]'
    $unclassified = 0
    foreach ($file in $Files) {
        if ($Options.Control.CancelRequested) { break }
        $Options.Control.Current = $file.Name
        $Options.Control.Phase = 'Organizing documents'
        $Options.Control.Completed++
        $Options.Control.Total = @($Files).Count
        if ($file.Extension.ToLowerInvariant() -notin @('.pdf','.doc','.docx','.ppt','.pptx','.xls','.xlsx','.odt','.odp','.epub')) { continue }
        Assert-PlainPath $file.FullName
        $course = Get-LmsCourse $file.Directory.Name $Profile.Courses
        $courseName = if ($course) { $course.Code } else { $file.Directory.Name }
        $classification = Get-LmsClassification $file.Name $courseName $Profile $Options.GroupBy $file.Name
        if (-not $classification.Folder) {
            Write-Host "  [Unclassified; unchanged] $($file.FullName)"
            $unclassified++
            $details.Add([pscustomobject]@{Source=$file.FullName; Target=$null; Action='Unclassified'; Reason=$classification.Reason; Course=$courseName})
            continue
        }
        $target = Get-ContainedPath $file.DirectoryName ($classification.Folder + '/' + $file.Name)
        $hash = $null
        if ($Options.Preview -and $Options.ExistingOnly -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target) -and -not $Planned.ContainsKey($target)) {
            # Existing-only previews need no content read when there is nothing to compare.
            # A real move still hashes the source for the undo record.
            $Planned[$target] = 'preview-source:' + $file.FullName
            $decision = [pscustomobject]@{Path=$target; Action='Write'; Comparison=''}
        } else {
            $hash = Get-FileDigest $file.FullName
            $decision = Get-LmsTarget $target $hash $Options.Conflict $Planned
        }
        $details.Add([pscustomobject]@{Source=$file.FullName; Target=$decision.Path; Action=$decision.Action; Reason=$classification.Reason; Course=$courseName; Comparison=$decision.Comparison; Bytes=$file.Length; Archive=$null})
        if ($decision.Comparison) { Write-Host "    $($decision.Comparison)" }
        if ($decision.Action -ne 'Write') { Write-Host "  [$($decision.Action); original kept] $($file.FullName)"; continue }
        Write-Host "  [Move] $($file.FullName) -> $($decision.Path)"
        Write-Host "    Why: $($classification.Reason)"
        if (-not $Options.Preview) {
            if (-not $journalPath) {
                $history = Get-ContainedPath $Root '.lms-unpacker/history'
                $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($history)
                $journalPath = Join-Path $history ((Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd-HHmmss') + '-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '.json')
            }
            $prefix = $Root.TrimEnd('\','/') + [IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
            $move = [pscustomobject]@{Source=$file.FullName.Substring($prefix.Length); Target=$decision.Path.Substring($prefix.Length); Hash=$hash; State='Pending'}
            $journal.Moves += $move
            Save-LmsJournal $journal $journalPath
            Assert-PlainPath $decision.Path
            $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($decision.Path))
            [IO.File]::Move($file.FullName,$decision.Path)
            $move.State = 'Moved'
            Save-LmsJournal $journal $journalPath
        }
        $moved++
    }
    if ($journalPath) { Write-Host "Undo record: $journalPath" }
    return [pscustomobject]@{Moved=$moved; Journal=$journalPath; Unclassified=$unclassified; Details=$details.ToArray()}
}

function Get-LmsScanFiles($InputItem, [int]$Depth) {
    Assert-PlainPath $InputItem.FullName
    if (-not $InputItem.PSIsContainer) { return $InputItem }
    $folders = @($InputItem)
    if ($Depth -eq 1) {
        $protected = @('Lab','Labs','submission','submissions','templates','Practice','needed','Syllabi','Lectures','Assignments','Quizzes and Exams','Books','Course Info','evidence','ScreenShots','node_modules')
        $folders += @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $InputItem.FullName -Directory | Where-Object {
            $_.Name -notin $protected -and -not $_.Name.StartsWith('.') -and -not ($_.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)
        })
    }
    foreach ($folder in $folders) {
        Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $folder.FullName -File | Where-Object { -not ($_.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) }
    }
}

function Find-LmsArchives {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Path, [ValidateRange(0,1)][int]$Depth=1)
    $inputItem=Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path -Force -ErrorAction Stop
    if (-not $inputItem.PSObject.Properties['FullName']) { throw 'Choose a filesystem folder or ZIP.' }
    Get-LmsScanFiles $inputItem $Depth | Where-Object { $_.Extension -eq '.zip' } | Sort-Object FullName
}

function Invoke-LmsUnpacker {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param(
        [string]$Path='.', [ValidateSet('Flatten','Extract','Organize')][string]$Mode='Flatten',
        [ValidateSet('Category','Week','Topic')][string]$GroupBy='Category',
        [ValidateRange(0,1)][int]$Depth=1, [ValidateSet('Skip','Rename','Overwrite')][string]$Conflict='Skip',
        [bool]$KeepZip=$false, [bool]$Preview=$false, [bool]$OrganizeExisting=$false,
        [bool]$ExistingOnly=$false, [string]$RulesPath, [string]$OutputPath,
        [hashtable]$Control, [hashtable]$PlannedTargets,
        [ValidateRange(1,100000)][int]$MaxEntries=10000,
        [ValidateRange(1,107374182400)][long]$MaxExpandedBytes=2GB
    )
    $options = @{} + $PSBoundParameters
    if (-not $Control) { $Control = [hashtable]::Synchronized(@{CancelRequested=$false; Current=''; Completed=0; Total=0; Phase=''}) }
    $options.Control = $Control
    $options.OutputPath = $null
    if ($OutputPath) {
        $options.OutputPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OutputPath)
        Assert-PlainPath $options.OutputPath
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $options.OutputPath -PathType Leaf) { throw 'Output path must be a folder.' }
        if ($ExistingOnly) { throw 'OutputPath applies to ZIP extraction only. Loose files are organized in place with undo.' }
    }
    foreach ($pair in @{Mode=$Mode; GroupBy=$GroupBy; Conflict=$Conflict; KeepZip=$KeepZip; Preview=$Preview; ExistingOnly=$ExistingOnly; MaxEntries=$MaxEntries; MaxExpandedBytes=$MaxExpandedBytes}.GetEnumerator()) { $options[$pair.Key] = $pair.Value }
    if (($OrganizeExisting -or $ExistingOnly) -and $Mode -ne 'Organize') { throw 'Use -Mode Organize with -OrganizeExisting or -ExistingOnly.' }
    if (($OrganizeExisting -or $ExistingOnly) -and $Conflict -eq 'Overwrite') { throw 'Existing-file organization supports Skip or Rename, not Overwrite.' }
    $profile = Read-LmsRules $RulesPath
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Path)) { throw "Selected folder or ZIP does not exist: $Path" }
    $inputItem = Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path -Force -ErrorAction Stop
    if (-not $inputItem -or -not $inputItem.PSObject.Properties['FullName']) { throw "Choose a filesystem folder or ZIP: $Path" }
    Assert-PlainPath $inputItem.FullName
    $files = @(Get-LmsScanFiles $inputItem $Depth)
    $root = $inputItem.FullName
    if (-not $inputItem.PSIsContainer) {
        if ($OrganizeExisting -or $ExistingOnly) { throw 'Existing-file organization requires a folder.' }
        if ($inputItem.Extension -ne '.zip') { throw 'Choose a folder or a .zip file.' }
        $files = @($inputItem)
        $root = $inputItem.DirectoryName
    }
    $zips = @($files | Where-Object { $_.Extension -eq '.zip' } | Sort-Object FullName)
    $options.Root = $root
    $options.Sources = @{}
    foreach ($zip in $zips) { $options.Sources[$zip.FullName] = $true }
    $result = [pscustomobject]@{Archives=0; Written=0; Skipped=0; Moved=0; Unclassified=0; Errors=0; Cancelled=$false; Preview=$Preview; Journal=$null; Details=@()}
    Write-Host "LMS Unpacker | Mode: $Mode | Conflicts: $Conflict | Keep ZIP: $KeepZip"
    if ($Mode -eq 'Organize') { Write-Host "Grouping: $GroupBy" }
    if ($Preview) { Write-Host 'PREVIEW: no files, folders or logs will be created, changed or deleted.' }
    if (-not $ExistingOnly -and -not $KeepZip) { Write-Host 'Successfully processed ZIPs will be deleted, including files skipped because their names already exist.' }
    $planned = if ($null -ne $PlannedTargets) { $PlannedTargets } else { @{} }
    if ($OrganizeExisting -or $ExistingOnly) {
        try {
            $organization = Invoke-LmsOrganization ($files | Sort-Object FullName) $root $options $profile $planned
            $result.Moved = $organization.Moved
            $result.Journal = $organization.Journal
            $result.Unclassified = $organization.Unclassified
            $result.Details += $organization.Details
        } catch {
            $result.Errors++
            Write-Host "[ERROR] Organization stopped: $($_.Exception.Message). Check .lms-unpacker/history for recovery. ZIP processing cancelled."
            return $result
        }
    }
    if (-not $ExistingOnly) {
        $Control.ArchiveIndex=0; $Control.ArchiveTotal=$zips.Count
        if ($zips.Count -eq 0) { Write-Host 'No ZIP files found in the selected scan scope.' }
        foreach ($zip in $zips) {
            if ($Control.CancelRequested) { break }
            $Control.ArchiveIndex++; $Control.Current=$zip.Name; $Control.Phase='Checking archive'; $Control.Completed=0; $Control.Total=0
            Write-Host "Archive: $($zip.FullName)"
            $archivePlan = @{} + $planned
            try {
                $unpacked = Invoke-LmsArchive $zip $options $profile $archivePlan
                $result.Archives++; $result.Written += $unpacked.Written; $result.Skipped += $unpacked.Skipped
                $result.Unclassified += $unpacked.Unclassified
                $result.Details += $unpacked.Details
                foreach ($key in $archivePlan.Keys) { $planned[$key] = $archivePlan[$key] }
            } catch [OperationCanceledException] { $result.Cancelled=$true; Write-Host $_.Exception.Message; break }
            catch { $result.Errors++; Write-Host "[ERROR; ZIP retained] $($_.Exception.Message)" }
        }
    }
    $result.Cancelled = $result.Cancelled -or [bool]$Control.CancelRequested
    Write-Host "Summary: $($result.Archives) ZIP(s), $($result.Written) file(s) written, $($result.Skipped) skipped, $($result.Moved) organized, $($result.Unclassified) unclassified, $($result.Errors) error(s)."
    return $result
}

function Undo-LmsOrganization {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$ReportPath, [switch]$Preview)
    $journal = Get-Content -LiteralPath $ReportPath -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
    if ($journal.Version -ne 1) { throw 'Unsupported undo record version.' }
    $root = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $journal.Root).FullName
    Assert-PlainPath $root
    $history = Get-ContainedPath $root '.lms-unpacker/history'
    $report = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $ReportPath).FullName
    if ([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($report) -ne $history) { throw 'The undo record must remain in its original .lms-unpacker/history folder.' }
    Assert-PlainPath $report
    $moves = @($journal.Moves)
    [array]::Reverse($moves)
    $restore = @()
    $recovered = @()
    foreach ($move in $moves) {
        if ($move.State -eq 'Undone') { continue }
        $source = Get-ContainedPath $root $move.Source
        $target = Get-ContainedPath $root $move.Target
        if ($move.State -eq 'Restoring' -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $source -PathType Leaf) -and
            -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target) -and (Get-FileDigest $source) -eq $move.Hash) {
            $recovered += $move
            continue
        }
        if ($move.State -eq 'Pending' -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $source -PathType Leaf) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target)) { continue }
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $source) { throw "Original location is occupied; nothing undone: $source" }
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $target -PathType Leaf) -or (Get-FileDigest $target) -ne $move.Hash) { throw "Organized file missing or modified; nothing undone: $target" }
        $restore += [pscustomobject]@{Source=$source; Target=$target; Move=$move}
    }
    if (-not $Preview -and $recovered.Count -gt 0) {
        foreach ($move in $recovered) { $move.State = 'Undone' }
        Save-LmsJournal $journal $report
    }
    foreach ($item in $restore) {
        Write-Host "[Restore] $($item.Target) -> $($item.Source)"
        if (-not $Preview) {
            Assert-PlainPath $item.Source
            Assert-PlainPath $item.Target
            $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($item.Source))
            $item.Move.State = 'Restoring'
            Save-LmsJournal $journal $report
            [IO.File]::Move($item.Target,$item.Source)
            $item.Move.State = 'Undone'
            Save-LmsJournal $journal $report
        }
    }
    Write-Host "Restored $($restore.Count) file(s). Empty category folders are kept."
}

function Test-LmsProfile {
    [CmdletBinding()]
    param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Path)
    $null = Read-LmsRules $Path
    return $true
}

New-CourseProfile.ps1

Converts courses/syllabus CSV rows into validated Version 1 JSON. Requires a new output filename and verifies rules before copying the result.

Read the full source (49 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
Build a reusable organization profile from course and syllabus CSV files.
.EXAMPLE
.\New-CourseProfile.ps1 -CoursesCsv .\courses.csv -SyllabusCsv .\syllabus.csv -OutputPath .\my-courses.json
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$CoursesCsv,
    [string]$SyllabusCsv,
    [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$OutputPath
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
try {
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $OutputPath) { throw 'Output already exists. Choose a new profile filename.' }
    $courses = @(Import-Csv -LiteralPath $CoursesCsv -Encoding UTF8)
    if ($courses.Count -eq 0) { throw 'Courses CSV is empty. Expected columns: Code,Folder,Name.' }
    $rules = @()
    if ($SyllabusCsv) {
        foreach ($row in (Import-Csv -LiteralPath $SyllabusCsv -Encoding UTF8)) {
            if (-not $row.Code -or -not $row.Pattern) { throw 'Syllabus rows need Code and Pattern.' }
            if ($row.Code -notin $courses.Code) { throw "Unknown syllabus course: $($row.Code)" }
            $folder = $row.Folder
            if (-not $folder) {
                $week = 0
                if (-not [int]::TryParse($row.Week,[ref]$week) -or $week -lt 1 -or $week -gt 99) { throw 'Supply Folder or a Week from 1 to 99 for every syllabus rule.' }
                $folder = 'Lectures/Week {0:D2}' -f $week
            }
            $rule = [pscustomobject]@{Course=$row.Code; Pattern=$row.Pattern; Folder=$folder}
            foreach ($optional in @('Topic','Reason')) {
                if ($row.PSObject.Properties[$optional] -and $row.$optional) { $rule | Add-Member NoteProperty $optional $row.$optional }
            }
            $rules += $rule
        }
    }
    $profile = [pscustomobject]@{Version=1; Courses=$courses; Rules=$rules}
    $output = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OutputPath)
    $temporary = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('lms-profile-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '.json')
    try {
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($temporary,($profile | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8),[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
        $null = Test-LmsProfile $temporary
        [IO.File]::Copy($temporary,$output,$false)
    } finally { if ([IO.File]::Exists($temporary)) { [IO.File]::Delete($temporary) } }
    Write-Host "Created profile: $output"
    Write-Host 'Use it with -Mode Organize -RulesPath, or choose My JSON profile in the launcher.'
} catch { Write-Host "[ERROR] $($_.Exception.Message)"; exit 1 }

packaging/LinuxLauncher.sh

Bash prefix used in the generated standalone Linux file. Checks pwsh, decodes the appended payload into mktemp storage, invokes the CLI and cleans up on exit.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (24 lines)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Self-contained application payload; requires an installed PowerShell 7 runtime.
set -euo pipefail
if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  printf '%s\n' 'LMS Unpacker requires PowerShell 7 (pwsh).' 'Install it using the official instructions: https://learn.microsoft.com/powershell/scripting/install/installing-powershell-on-linux' >&2
  exit 127
fi
runtime="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/lms-unpacker.XXXXXXXX")"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$runtime"' EXIT
awk 'payload { print } /^__LMS_PAYLOAD__$/ { payload=1 }' "$0" | base64 --decode > "$runtime/app.zip"
cat > "$runtime/launch.ps1" <<'POWERSHELL'
param([Parameter(ValueFromRemainingArguments=$true)][string[]]$LaunchArguments)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
try {
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::ExtractToDirectory((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'app.zip'),(Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'app'))
    & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'app/Start-Cli.ps1') @LaunchArguments
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
} catch { Write-Error $_; exit 1 }
POWERSHELL
pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -File "$runtime/launch.ps1" "$@"
exit $?
__LMS_PAYLOAD__

param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$OutputPath)
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
$frames = @()
foreach ($size in @(16,32,48,256)) {
    $bitmap = [Drawing.Bitmap]::new($size,$size)
    $graphics = [Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bitmap)
    $graphics.SmoothingMode = 'AntiAlias'
    $graphics.Clear([Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(20,99,75))
    $scale = $size / 32.0
    $pen = [Drawing.Pen]::new([Drawing.Color]::White,([single](1.8*$scale)))
    $gold = [Drawing.Pen]::new([Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(245,207,117),([single](2*$scale)))
    $points = [Drawing.PointF[]]@([Drawing.PointF]::new(7*$scale,13*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(13*$scale,13*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(16*$scale,16*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(25*$scale,16*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(25*$scale,26*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(7*$scale,26*$scale),[Drawing.PointF]::new(7*$scale,13*$scale))
    $graphics.DrawLines($pen,$points)
    $graphics.DrawLine($gold,[single](16*$scale),[single](5*$scale),[single](16*$scale),[single](18*$scale))
    $graphics.DrawLine($gold,[single](12*$scale),[single](14*$scale),[single](16*$scale),[single](18*$scale))
    $graphics.DrawLine($gold,[single](20*$scale),[single](14*$scale),[single](16*$scale),[single](18*$scale))
    $buffer = [IO.MemoryStream]::new()
    $bitmap.Save($buffer,[Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png)
    $frames += [pscustomobject]@{Size=$size; Bytes=$buffer.ToArray()}
    $buffer.Dispose(); $gold.Dispose(); $pen.Dispose(); $graphics.Dispose(); $bitmap.Dispose()
}
$output = [IO.File]::Create($OutputPath)
$writer = [IO.BinaryWriter]::new($output)
try {
    $writer.Write([uint16]0); $writer.Write([uint16]1); $writer.Write([uint16]$frames.Count)
    $offset = 6 + 16*$frames.Count
    foreach ($frame in $frames) {
        $dimension = if ($frame.Size -eq 256) { 0 } else { $frame.Size }
        $writer.Write([byte]$dimension); $writer.Write([byte]$dimension); $writer.Write([byte]0); $writer.Write([byte]0)
        $writer.Write([uint16]1); $writer.Write([uint16]32); $writer.Write([uint32]$frame.Bytes.Length); $writer.Write([uint32]$offset)
        $offset += $frame.Bytes.Length
    }
    foreach ($frame in $frames) { $writer.Write([byte[]]$frame.Bytes) }
} finally { $writer.Dispose(); $output.Dispose() }

packaging/PortableHost.cs

Small .NET Framework entry point compiled as console or windowed host. Extracts its own embedded resource, transports arguments as Base64 JSON, starts PowerShell, waits and cleans owned temp storage.

Main

C# host entry point. Extracts embedded App.zip into a unique contained temp root, launches fixed PowerShell script with Base64-JSON data arguments, waits/propagates exit status, reports startup errors and cleans owned runtime storage. DESKTOP/CLI compile constants choose presentation.

Declared at line 20
Read the full source (99 lines)
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text;
using System.Web.Script.Serialization;
using System.Windows.Forms;

[assembly: AssemblyTitle("LMS Unpacker")]
[assembly: AssemblyDescription("Portable offline ZIP extraction and course organization")]
[assembly: AssemblyCompany("LMS Unpacker contributors")]
[assembly: AssemblyProduct("LMS Unpacker")]
[assembly: AssemblyVersion("1.4.1.0")]

// Both editions carry the same offline payload. No installer or download at startup.
internal static class PortableHost
{
    [STAThread]
    private static int Main(string[] args)
    {
        string temporary = null;
        int result = 1;
        try
        {
            string parent = Path.GetFullPath(Path.GetTempPath());
            temporary = Path.Combine(parent, "lms-unpacker-" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N"));
            Directory.CreateDirectory(temporary);
            using (Stream stream = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetManifestResourceStream("App.zip"))
            using (ZipArchive zip = new ZipArchive(stream, ZipArchiveMode.Read))
            {
                string prefix = temporary + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
                foreach (ZipArchiveEntry entry in zip.Entries)
                {
                    string target = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(temporary, entry.FullName));
                    if (!target.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        throw new InvalidDataException("Invalid application package path.");
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(target));
                    if (entry.Name.Length != 0) entry.ExtractToFile(target, false);
                }
            }
            string root = Path.Combine(temporary, "LMS-Unpacker");
            string engine = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Windows),
                @"System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe");
            if (!File.Exists(engine)) throw new FileNotFoundException("Windows PowerShell 5.1 is required.");
            ProcessStartInfo start = new ProcessStartInfo(engine);
#if DESKTOP
            string script = "Start-Desktop.ps1";
            start.CreateNoWindow = true;
            start.WindowStyle = ProcessWindowStyle.Hidden;
            start.RedirectStandardError = true;
#else
            string script = "Start-Cli.ps1";
#endif
            start.UseShellExecute = false;
            start.Arguments = "-NoLogo -NoProfile -STA -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"" + Path.Combine(root, script) + "\"";
            // Arguments are data, never interpolated into a PowerShell command or a shell.
            start.EnvironmentVariables["LMS_ARGUMENTS"] = Convert.ToBase64String(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(new JavaScriptSerializer().Serialize(args)));
            start.EnvironmentVariables["LMS_APP_EXE"] = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location;
            using (Process child = Process.Start(start))
            {
#if DESKTOP
                string diagnostics = child.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
#endif
                child.WaitForExit(); result = child.ExitCode;
#if DESKTOP
                if (result != 0 && !String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(diagnostics))
                    MessageBox.Show(diagnostics, "LMS Unpacker could not start", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
#endif
            }
        }
        catch (Exception error)
        {
#if DESKTOP
            MessageBox.Show(error.Message + "\n\nTry a writable local folder. On a managed computer, ask IT about application restrictions.",
                "LMS Unpacker could not open", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
#else
            Console.Error.WriteLine("[ERROR] " + error.Message);
#endif
        }
        finally
        {
            if (temporary != null)
            {
                string prefix = Path.GetFullPath(Path.GetTempPath()).TrimEnd(Path.DirectorySeparatorChar) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
                string resolved = Path.GetFullPath(temporary);
                if (resolved.StartsWith(prefix, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    Path.GetFileName(resolved).StartsWith("lms-unpacker-", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    try { Directory.Delete(resolved, true); }
                    catch (IOException) { /* A viewer may still hold an embedded help file open. */ }
                    catch (UnauthorizedAccessException) { }
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    }
}

ProfileEditor.ps1

WPF course/rule editor using DataTables. Saves a validated new JSON copy, optionally seeded from a selected unclassified result.

Save-VisualProfile

Inputs: new destination and course/rule rows. Serializes a temporary profile, validates through the shared engine and copies without overwrite. Finally deletes its owned temporary JSON.

Declared at line 2

Show-ProfileEditor

Inputs: owner, current profile, optional seed and test Inspect callback. Creates course/rule DataTables, binds grids, validates and saves a new copy. Returns its selected output path; errors remain visible in the editor.

Declared at line 13
Read the full source (67 lines)
# Visual editor; saves a validated copy so shipped profiles and previous versions survive.
function Save-VisualProfile([string]$Destination, $Courses, $Rules) {
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
    $model = @{Version=1; Courses=@($Courses); Rules=@($Rules)}
    $temporary = Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('lms-profile-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N') + '.json')
    try {
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($temporary,($model | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8),[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
        $null = Test-LmsProfile $temporary
        [IO.File]::Copy($temporary,$Destination,$false)
    } finally { if ([IO.File]::Exists($temporary)) { [IO.File]::Delete($temporary) } }
}

function Show-ProfileEditor($Owner, [string]$ProfilePath, $Seed, [scriptblock]$Inspect) {
    [xml]$markup = @'
<Window xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation" xmlns:x="http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml" Title="Course and syllabus editor" Width="940" Height="640" MinWidth="700" MinHeight="460" WindowStartupLocation="CenterOwner" FontFamily="Segoe UI" FontSize="14">
 <Grid Margin="18"><Grid.RowDefinitions><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/><RowDefinition Height="*"/><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/><RowDefinition Height="Auto"/></Grid.RowDefinitions>
 <TextBlock Text="Create rules for any course. Add rows at the bottom; select a row and press Delete to remove it. Save a copy, then preview before applying." TextWrapping="Wrap" Margin="0,0,0,14"/>
 <TabControl Grid.Row="1">
  <TabItem Header="_Courses"><DataGrid x:Name="Courses" AutoGenerateColumns="False" CanUserAddRows="True" CanUserDeleteRows="True" AutomationProperties.Name="Course mappings"><DataGrid.Columns>
   <DataGridTextColumn Header="Code (e.g. BIO-101)" Binding="{Binding Code}" Width="180"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Folder (e.g. Biology)" Binding="{Binding Folder}" Width="220"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Course name" Binding="{Binding Name}" Width="*"/>
  </DataGrid.Columns></DataGrid></TabItem>
  <TabItem Header="_Syllabus rules"><DataGrid x:Name="Rules" AutoGenerateColumns="False" CanUserAddRows="True" CanUserDeleteRows="True" AutomationProperties.Name="Filename and syllabus rules"><DataGrid.Columns>
   <DataGridTextColumn Header="Course code or *" Binding="{Binding Course}" Width="120"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Filename pattern" Binding="{Binding Pattern}" Width="180"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Category / week folder" Binding="{Binding Folder}" Width="190"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Topic (optional)" Binding="{Binding Topic}" Width="150"/><DataGridTextColumn Header="Reason (optional)" Binding="{Binding Reason}" Width="200"/>
  </DataGrid.Columns></DataGrid></TabItem>
 </TabControl>
 <TextBlock Grid.Row="2" Margin="0,12,0,12" TextWrapping="Wrap" Text="Example: BIO-101 | *cell* | Lectures/Week 02 | Cell biology. Use Assignments or Books for other categories. * matches any text. Rules match filenames, first match wins. New review rules are inserted first. Unknown weeks can use Lectures/Unscheduled."/>
 <DockPanel Grid.Row="3"><Button x:Name="Save" Content="_Save profile copy..." Padding="16,10" DockPanel.Dock="Right"/><TextBlock x:Name="Error" TextWrapping="Wrap" VerticalAlignment="Center" Margin="0,0,12,0" AutomationProperties.LiveSetting="Polite"/></DockPanel>
 </Grid>
</Window>
'@
    $editor = [Windows.Markup.XamlReader]::Load([Xml.XmlNodeReader]::new($markup))
    if (-not $Inspect) { $editor.Owner = $Owner }
    $editor.Width = [Math]::Min(940,[Windows.SystemParameters]::WorkArea.Width - 30)
    $editor.Height = [Math]::Min(640,[Windows.SystemParameters]::WorkArea.Height - 30)
    $coursesTable = [Data.DataTable]::new()
    foreach ($column in @('Code','Folder','Name')) { $null = $coursesTable.Columns.Add($column,[string]) }
    $rulesTable = [Data.DataTable]::new()
    foreach ($column in @('Course','Pattern','Folder','Topic','Reason')) { $null = $rulesTable.Columns.Add($column,[string]) }
    if ($ProfilePath) {
        $null = Test-LmsProfile $ProfilePath
        $profile = Get-Content -LiteralPath $ProfilePath -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
        foreach ($course in $profile.Courses) { $row=$coursesTable.NewRow(); foreach ($c in $coursesTable.Columns) { $row[$c.ColumnName]=[string]$course.($c.ColumnName) }; $coursesTable.Rows.Add($row) }
    } else { $profile = @{Rules=@()} }
    $rules = @()
    if ($Seed) { $rules += $Seed }
    $rules += @($profile.Rules)
    foreach ($rule in $rules) { $row=$rulesTable.NewRow(); foreach ($c in $rulesTable.Columns) { $row[$c.ColumnName]=[string]$rule.($c.ColumnName) }; $rulesTable.Rows.Add($row) }
    $courseGrid = $editor.FindName('Courses'); $ruleGrid = $editor.FindName('Rules')
    $courseGrid.ItemsSource = $coursesTable.DefaultView; $ruleGrid.ItemsSource = $rulesTable.DefaultView
    if ($Seed) { $ruleGrid.Parent.IsSelected=$true }
    $saved = @{Path=$null}
    $editor.FindName('Save').Add_Click({
        try {
            foreach ($grid in @($courseGrid,$ruleGrid)) { if (-not $grid.CommitEdit([Windows.Controls.DataGridEditingUnit]::Cell,$true) -or -not $grid.CommitEdit([Windows.Controls.DataGridEditingUnit]::Row,$true)) { throw 'Finish the current row before saving.' } }
            $courses = @($coursesTable.Rows | Where-Object { $_.RowState -ne 'Deleted' } | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{Code=[string]$_.Code; Folder=[string]$_.Folder; Name=[string]$_.Name} })
            $rules = @($rulesTable.Rows | Where-Object { $_.RowState -ne 'Deleted' } | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{Course=[string]$_.Course; Pattern=[string]$_.Pattern; Folder=[string]$_.Folder; Topic=[string]$_.Topic; Reason=[string]$_.Reason} })
            $dialog=[Microsoft.Win32.SaveFileDialog]::new(); $dialog.Filter='JSON profile (*.json)|*.json'; $dialog.FileName='my-courses.json'; $dialog.Title='Save as a new profile file'
            if (-not $dialog.ShowDialog($editor)) { return }
            Save-VisualProfile $dialog.FileName $courses $rules
            $saved.Path=$dialog.FileName; $editor.DialogResult=$true
        } catch { $editor.FindName('Error').Text=$_.Exception.Message }
    })
    if ($Inspect) { & $Inspect $editor $coursesTable $rulesTable; return }
    $null=$editor.ShowDialog()
    return $saved.Path
}

Run-Unpack.bat

Source-distribution Windows guided-menu launcher. Checks required siblings, selects PowerShell, passes one input path and pauses for readable output. It is not the standalone EXE.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (50 lines)
@echo off
setlocal DisableDelayedExpansion
if /i "%~1"=="/?" goto help
if /i "%~1"=="--help" goto help
if not "%~2"=="" (
  echo Drop one folder or ZIP at a time, or launch this file without arguments.
  pause
  exit /b 1
)
for %%F in (Start-Unpacker.ps1 LmsUnpacker.psm1 ZipIntegrity.cs) do if not exist "%~dp0%%F" goto missing
where /q pwsh.exe >nul 2>nul
if not errorlevel 1 (
  set "lms_engine=pwsh.exe"
) else (
  set "lms_engine=%SystemRoot%\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
)
if not exist "%lms_engine%" if not "%lms_engine%"=="pwsh.exe" goto noengine
"%lms_engine%" -NoLogo -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0Start-Unpacker.ps1" -Path "%~1"
set "lms_exit=%errorlevel%"
if not "%lms_exit%"=="0" (
  echo.
  echo The launcher reported an error. Read the message above.
  echo Open START-HERE.html or LAUNCH-HELP.txt for launch and troubleshooting steps.
)
echo.
pause
exit /b %lms_exit%

:missing
echo Some app files are missing. Extract the entire release ZIP first.
echo Keep Run-Unpack.bat, Start-Unpacker.ps1, LmsUnpacker.psm1 and ZipIntegrity.cs together.
pause
exit /b 1

:noengine
echo PowerShell was not found. This app requires Windows PowerShell 5.1 or PowerShell 7.
echo See LAUNCH-HELP.txt. A managed computer may restrict PowerShell.
pause
exit /b 1

:help
echo LMS Unpacker - quick start
echo Double-click Run-Unpack.bat, or drop one folder or ZIP onto it.
echo Read START-HERE.html for the visual tutorial.
echo If BAT files do not open, paste this command into PowerShell:
echo powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File "%~dp0Start-Unpacker.ps1"
echo This applies execution policy only to the new process; it does not change your account policy.
echo See LAUNCH-HELP.txt for other launch options.
exit /b 0

Run-Unpack.sh

Source-distribution Bash entry point that checks for pwsh and dispatches into the repository CLI.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (9 lines)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  printf '%s\n' 'LMS Unpacker requires PowerShell 7 (pwsh).' 'Install it using the official instructions: https://learn.microsoft.com/powershell/scripting/install/installing-powershell-on-linux' >&2
  exit 127
fi
root="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")" && pwd)"
exec pwsh -NoLogo -NoProfile -File "$root/Start-Cli.ps1" "$@"

SECURITY.md

Sign-Release.ps1

Optional local Authenticode helper for an existing certificate. Validates thumbprint, validity/private key/EKU/trust, signs EXEs and refreshes hashes. Does not obtain a certificate.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (28 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Thumbprint,
    [string]$TimestampServer,
    [switch]$AllowUntrustedForTesting
)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$thumb=$Thumbprint.Replace(' ','')
if ($thumb -notmatch '^[A-Fa-f0-9]{40}$') { throw 'Supply the SHA-1 thumbprint of your existing code-signing certificate.' }
$certificate=Get-Item -LiteralPath "Cert:\CurrentUser\My\$thumb"
if (-not $certificate.HasPrivateKey -or $certificate.NotAfter -le (Get-Date) -or $certificate.NotBefore -gt (Get-Date)) { throw 'A current certificate with its private key is required.' }
$usages=@($certificate.Extensions | Where-Object { $_.Oid.Value -eq '2.5.29.37' } | ForEach-Object { $_.EnhancedKeyUsages } | ForEach-Object { $_.Value })
if ('1.3.6.1.5.5.7.3.3' -notin $usages) { throw 'Certificate must have the Code Signing usage.' }
if (-not $certificate.Verify() -and -not $AllowUntrustedForTesting) { throw 'Certificate chain is not trusted. Use -AllowUntrustedForTesting only for a private development build; public users will not trust this signature automatically.' }
foreach ($name in @('LMS-Unpacker.exe','LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe')) {
    $path=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('dist/'+$name)
    $options=@{FilePath=$path; Certificate=$certificate; HashAlgorithm='SHA256'}
    if ($TimestampServer) { $options.TimestampServer=$TimestampServer }
    $signature=Set-AuthenticodeSignature @options
    if (-not $signature.SignerCertificate -or $signature.SignerCertificate.Thumbprint -ne $thumb -or
        ($signature.Status -ne 'Valid' -and -not ($AllowUntrustedForTesting -and $signature.Status -eq 'NotTrusted'))) { throw "Signing failed for $name : $($signature.Status)" }
    $hash=(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $path -Algorithm SHA256).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText(($path+'.sha256'),($hash+'  '+$name+[Environment]::NewLine))
    Write-Host "$name : $($signature.Status)"
}
Write-Host 'Checksums refreshed. Signing changes the bytes: previous GitHub attestations do not apply to these local signed files.'

Start-Cli.ps1

Allowlisted argument dispatcher for help/menu/tutorial/profile/undo or direct named options. Decodes host arguments as data and propagates failure exit codes.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (75 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
param([Parameter(ValueFromRemainingArguments=$true)][string[]]$LaunchArguments)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
try {
    $arguments = @($LaunchArguments | Where-Object { $null -ne $_ })
    if ($env:LMS_ARGUMENTS) { [string[]]$arguments = ([Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString([Convert]::FromBase64String($env:LMS_ARGUMENTS)) | ConvertFrom-Json) }
    if ($arguments.Count -eq 0 -or $arguments[0] -eq '--menu') {
        if ($arguments.Count -gt 2) { throw 'Use --menu with at most one folder or ZIP path.' }
        $selected = if ($arguments.Count -gt 1) { $arguments[1] } else { $null }
        & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Start-Unpacker.ps1') -Path $selected
        exit $LASTEXITCODE
    }
    if ($arguments[0] -in @('--help','-h','/?')) {
        Write-Host @'
LMS Unpacker CLI
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe                           Guided menu
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe --menu "C:\Courses"        Menu with a selected folder
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe -Path "C:\Courses" -DryRun Preview default flattening
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe -Path "C:\a.zip" -Mode Extract
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe -Path "C:\Courses" -Mode Organize -GroupBy Topic -RulesPath NUST -ExistingOnly -DryRun
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe --undo "C:\Courses\.lms-unpacker\history\record.json" -DryRun
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe --profile                 Build a profile from your CSVs
  LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe --tutorial                Open the offline visual guide

Defaults: Flatten; Skip existing filenames; DELETE ZIPs after success, including skips.
Use -KeepZip to retain ZIPs, or -Conflict Rename to keep different versions.
Named options run directly. Review with -DryRun first; remove it to apply.
-Mode Flatten|Extract|Organize   -GroupBy Category|Week|Topic   -Depth 0|1
-Conflict Skip|Rename|Overwrite -RulesPath NUST|<JSON path>    -KeepZip
-OutputPath <folder>            ZIP output only; loose files stay in place
-OrganizeExisting  -ExistingOnly  -DryRun / -WhatIf  -MaxEntries N  -MaxExpandedGB N
Only existing-file organization can be undone. ZIP deletion is permanent.
From PowerShell, prefix a quoted executable path with &. No separate files are needed.
Linux: bash LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh -Path "$HOME/Courses" -DryRun (requires pwsh 7).
'@
        exit 0
    }
    if ($arguments[0] -eq '--tutorial') {
        Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'START-HERE.html')
        $null = Read-Host 'Press Enter when you have finished with the guide (temporary help files will be removed)'
        exit 0
    }
    if ($arguments[0] -eq '--profile') {
        $courseCsv = (Read-Host 'Courses CSV path').Trim().Trim('"')
        $syllabusCsv = (Read-Host 'Syllabus CSV path (Enter to omit)').Trim().Trim('"')
        $output = (Read-Host 'Save profile JSON to (new filename)').Trim().Trim('"')
        & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'New-CourseProfile.ps1') -CoursesCsv $courseCsv -SyllabusCsv $syllabusCsv -OutputPath $output
        exit $LASTEXITCODE
    }
    if ($arguments[0] -eq '--undo') {
        if ($arguments.Count -lt 2 -or $arguments.Count -gt 3 -or ($arguments.Count -eq 3 -and $arguments[2] -ne '-DryRun')) { throw 'Use --undo <record.json> [-DryRun].' }
        Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
        Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $arguments[1] -Preview:($arguments.Count -eq 3)
        exit 0
    }
    $switches = @('KeepZip','DryRun','WhatIf','OrganizeExisting','ExistingOnly','SkipExisting','Overwrite','PassThru')
    $values = @('Path','Mode','GroupBy','Depth','Conflict','RulesPath','OutputPath','MaxEntries','MaxExpandedGB')
    $options = @{}
    for ($index = 0; $index -lt $arguments.Count; $index++) {
        $name = [string]$arguments[$index]
        if (-not $name.StartsWith('-')) { throw 'Use -Path before a folder or ZIP path. Use --help for examples.' }
        $name = $name.TrimStart('-')
        if ($options.ContainsKey($name)) { throw "Duplicate option: $name" }
        if ($name -in $switches) { $options[$name] = $true }
        elseif ($name -in $values) {
            $index++
            if ($index -ge $arguments.Count) { throw "Missing value for -$name" }
            $options[$name] = $arguments[$index]
        } else { throw "Unknown option: -$name. Use --help." }
    }
    if ($options.RulesPath -eq 'NUST') { $options.RulesPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\nust.json' }
    & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Unpack-Lms.ps1') @options
    exit $LASTEXITCODE
} catch { Write-Host "[ERROR] $($_.Exception.Message)"; exit 1 }

Start-Desktop.ps1

WPF composition root. Loads markup/modules, binds named controls, restores settings, converts options, owns runspaces/timers and handles operation results/startup/closing.

Show-Problem

Input: message. Sets persistent status and displays an owner-bound warning dialog for a user-action error.

Declared at line 49

Update-Hints

Synchronizes task/group/default explanations, queue count, organization-only controls and applicable result columns. Does not execute a plan.

Declared at line 53

Invalidate-Preview

When idle, clears current preview/undo state, disables Apply, resets feedback and refreshes hints/discovery. Called by relevant setting/path/queue changes; busy operations keep their snapshot.

Declared at line 76

Get-Options

Reads/validates current UI choices and maps task indices to engine parameters plus QueuePaths. Rejects missing inputs, wrong file types and unsupported loose-only input choices. Returns a hashtable snapshot.

Declared at line 91

Set-Busy

Input: boolean. Changes editability, preview/apply/cancel/open actions and progress visibility consistently while preserving relevant classification enablement. All controls remain on the UI dispatcher thread.

Declared at line 110

Save-Preferences

Persists last path, independent picker memories and workspaces to LocalAppData JSON, excluding generated Hint fields. A save failure is reported in activity rather than changing extraction results.

Declared at line 125

Start-Work

Inputs: operation, options and optional undo path. Initializes shared progress, starts feedback, disables mutations and begins a separate PowerShell runspace. Its script rejects overlapping scopes, processes a shared collision plan and stops the queue on error/cancel.

Declared at line 133

Read-Activity

Updates progress and appends only new worker information-stream entries using a retained cursor. Called by the dispatcher timer, not the background engine.
