#requires -Version 5.1
[CmdletBinding()]
param([string]$Path, [switch]$SmokeTest, [string]$RenderPath)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Add-Type -AssemblyName PresentationFramework,PresentationCore,WindowsBase,System.Windows.Forms

try {
    $reader = [Xml.XmlReader]::Create((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Desktop.xaml'))
    try { $window = [Windows.Markup.XamlReader]::Load($reader) } finally { $reader.Dispose() }
    if ([IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'App.ico'))) { $window.Icon = [Windows.Media.Imaging.BitmapFrame]::Create([uri](Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'App.ico')) }
    $ui = @{}
    foreach ($name in @('HelpButton','StartMenuButton','RemoveShortcutButton','PathPanel','InputPath','FolderButton','ZipButton',
        'SettingsPanel','TaskChoice','TaskHint','OrganizationPanel','ProfileChoice','CustomProfilePanel','ProfilePath','ProfileBrowseButton',
        'CreateProfileButton','GroupChoice','GroupHint','ConflictChoice','KeepZip','ScanChildren','DefaultsNotice','PreviewButton','UndoButton',
        'SummaryText','ResultsTabs','PlanGrid','ActivityText','JournalText','StatusText','BusyProgress','OpenFolderButton','ApplyButton',
        'WorkspaceChoice','WorkspaceName','SaveWorkspaceButton','RemoveWorkspaceButton','QueueList','QueueAddButton','QueueClearButton','QueueFirstButton','QueueRemoveButton','QueueUpButton','QueueDownButton','FileDetails',
        'EditProfileButton','ClassifyButton','OutputPath','OutputBrowseButton','CancelButton','UnclassifiedOnly','QueueExpander','FeedbackPanel','FeedbackTitle','WorkItemText','ResultOpenButton',
        'WorkspaceExpander','OptionsExpander','AppExpander','ReviewTools','DiscoveryText','AppFolderButton','RescanButton','ArchivesTab','ArchiveGrid','UseDetectedButton')) {
        $ui[$name] = $window.FindName($name)
        if (-not $ui[$name]) { throw "Missing interface control: $name" }
    }
    $state = @{ Busy=$false; Preview=$null; Operation=$null; Worker=$null; Handle=$null; InformationIndex=0; UndoPath=$null; Workspaces=@(); LastFolderPicker=''; LastZipPicker=''; DiscoveryVersion=0; DiscoveryWorker=$null; DiscoveryPending=$null }
    $preferences = Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('LocalApplicationData')) 'LMS Unpacker\preferences.json'
    $shortcut = Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('Programs')) 'LMS Unpacker.lnk'
    $appExe = $env:LMS_APP_EXE
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'DesktopPaths.psm1') -Force
    $state.LaunchFolder=Get-LmsLaunchFolder $appExe (Get-Location).Path
    $savedPreferences=$null
    if (-not $SmokeTest -and [IO.File]::Exists($preferences)) {
        try {
            $savedPreferences=Get-Content -LiteralPath $preferences -Raw -Encoding UTF8 | ConvertFrom-Json
            $state.Workspaces=@($savedPreferences.Workspaces | Where-Object { $_ -and $_.Name })
            $state.LastFolderPicker=if ($savedPreferences.LastFolderPicker) { $savedPreferences.LastFolderPicker } else { $savedPreferences.Path }
            $state.LastZipPicker=if ($savedPreferences.LastZipPicker) { $savedPreferences.LastZipPicker } else { $savedPreferences.Path }
            $ui.WorkspaceChoice.ItemsSource=@($state.Workspaces)
        } catch { $state.Workspaces=@() }
    }
    $window.Width = [Math]::Min(1120,[Windows.SystemParameters]::WorkArea.Width - 30)
    $window.Height = [Math]::Min(790,[Windows.SystemParameters]::WorkArea.Height - 30)
    if (-not [Windows.SystemParameters]::HighContrast) {
        $window.Background = [Windows.Media.BrushConverter]::new().ConvertFromString('#F5F4EF')
        $window.Foreground = [Windows.Media.BrushConverter]::new().ConvertFromString('#182D2B')
        foreach ($name in @('ApplyButton')) {
            $ui[$name].Background = [Windows.Media.BrushConverter]::new().ConvertFromString('#14634B')
            $ui[$name].Foreground = [Windows.Media.Brushes]::White
        }
    }

    function Show-Problem([string]$Message) {
        $ui.StatusText.Text = $Message
        $null = [Windows.MessageBox]::Show($window,$Message,'LMS Unpacker','OK','Warning')
    }
    function Update-Hints {
        $ui.QueueExpander.Header = 'Additional inputs (' + $ui.QueueList.Items.Count + ')'
        $organize = $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ge 2
        $ui.OrganizationPanel.Visibility = if ($organize) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.ReviewTools.Visibility = if ($organize -and $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.PlanGrid.Columns[4].Visibility=if ($organize) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.PlanGrid.Columns[3].Visibility=if (@($ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource | Where-Object { $_.Comparison }).Count) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.CustomProfilePanel.Visibility = if ($ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 2) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.KeepZip.IsEnabled = $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ne 3
        $ui.OutputPath.IsEnabled = $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ne 3
        $ui.OutputBrowseButton.IsEnabled = $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ne 3
        $ui.TaskHint.Text = @('Remove LMS wrapper folders. Files land directly beside each ZIP.',
            'Keep every internal folder inside a folder named after the ZIP. Best for projects and linked files.',
            'Sort ZIP contents and loose course documents into useful folders. Working subfolders are not scanned recursively.',
            'Organize already-extracted documents. No ZIPs are opened or deleted.')[ $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex ]
        $ui.GroupHint.Text = @('Assignments, books and other categories stay separate.',
            'Use week labels or syllabus rules. Broad topics may span weeks; uncertain weeks stay Unscheduled.',
            'Topic names come from your profile. Lectures without a matching topic go to Unsorted topics.')[ $ui.GroupChoice.SelectedIndex ]
        $ui.DefaultsNotice.Text = if ($ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 3) { 'Existing documents are never overwritten. An undo record is saved when files move.' }
            elseif ($ui.KeepZip.IsChecked) { 'Source ZIPs will be kept after success.' }
            elseif ($ui.ConflictChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 0) { 'After success, ZIPs are permanently deleted, including incoming files skipped because their names already exist.' }
            else { 'Different versions get new filenames. Source ZIPs are permanently deleted after success.' }
    }
    function Invalidate-Preview {
        if ($state.Busy) { return }
        $state.Preview = $null
        $state.UndoPath = $null
        $ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled = $false
        $ui.ApplyButton.Content = '_Apply changes'
        $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource = $null
        $ui.ClassifyButton.IsEnabled=$false
        $ui.SummaryText.Text = 'Selection or settings changed. Preview to see the current plan.'
        $ui.StatusText.Text = 'Ready • Preview first'
        Reset-Feedback
        $ui.OpenFolderButton.IsEnabled = -not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ui.InputPath.Text)
        Update-Hints
        if (-not $state.UpdatingDiscovery) { Request-ZipDiscovery }
    }
    function Get-Options {
        $selectedPath = $ui.InputPath.Text.Trim().Trim('"')
        if (-not $selectedPath) { throw 'Choose a folder or ZIP first.' }
        $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $selectedPath
        if (-not $item.PSIsContainer -and $item.Extension -ne '.zip') { throw 'Choose a folder or a ZIP file.' }
        if (-not $item.PSIsContainer -and $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 3) { throw 'Choose a folder for existing-document organization.' }
        $rules = if ($ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ge 2) { switch ($ui.ProfileChoice.SelectedIndex) {
            1 { Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\nust.json' }
            2 { if (-not $ui.ProfilePath.Text.Trim()) { throw 'Choose your JSON profile, or use General.' }; $ui.ProfilePath.Text.Trim().Trim('"') }
            default { $null }
        } } else { $null }
        return @{
            Path=$item.FullName; Mode=@('Flatten','Extract','Organize','Organize')[$ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex]
            QueuePaths=@($ui.QueueList.Items); OutputPath=$(if ($ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ne 3) { $ui.OutputPath.Text.Trim().Trim('"') } else { '' })
            GroupBy=@('Category','Week','Topic')[$ui.GroupChoice.SelectedIndex]; Depth=[int][bool]$ui.ScanChildren.IsChecked
            Conflict=@('Skip','Rename')[$ui.ConflictChoice.SelectedIndex]; KeepZip=[bool]$ui.KeepZip.IsChecked; RulesPath=$rules
            OrganizeExisting=($ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 2 -and $item.PSIsContainer); ExistingOnly=($ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 3)
        }
    }
    function Set-Busy([bool]$Busy) {
        $state.Busy = $Busy
        $ui.PathPanel.IsEnabled = -not $Busy
        $ui.SettingsPanel.IsEnabled = -not $Busy
        foreach ($name in @('UndoButton','StartMenuButton','RemoveShortcutButton','ClassifyButton','PreviewButton')) { $ui[$name].IsEnabled = -not $Busy }
        $ui.ClassifyButton.IsEnabled = -not $Busy -and $ui.TaskChoice.SelectedIndex -ge 2 -and $null -ne $ui.PlanGrid.SelectedItem
        $ui.UseDetectedButton.IsEnabled = -not $Busy -and $ui.ArchiveGrid.SelectedItems.Count -gt 0
        $ui.CancelButton.Visibility = if ($Busy -and $state.Operation -notlike 'Undo*') { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.CancelButton.IsEnabled = $true
        $ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled = $false
        $ui.BusyProgress.Visibility = if ($Busy) { 'Visible' } else { 'Collapsed' }
        $ui.OpenFolderButton.IsEnabled = -not $Busy -and -not [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($ui.InputPath.Text)
        $ui.PreviewButton.Content = if ($Busy -and $state.Operation -like '*Preview') { 'Reading files...' } else { '_Preview changes' }
        if ($Busy -and $state.Operation -like '*Apply') { $ui.ApplyButton.Content='Working...' }
    }
    function Save-Preferences {
        try {
            $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($preferences))
            $remember = $ui.InputPath.Text.Trim().Trim('"')
            if (-not [IO.Directory]::Exists($remember)) { $remember = [IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($remember) }
            [IO.File]::WriteAllText($preferences,(@{Path=$remember; Workspaces=@($state.Workspaces | Select-Object * -ExcludeProperty Hint); LastFolderPicker=$state.LastFolderPicker; LastZipPicker=$state.LastZipPicker} | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8),[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
        } catch { $ui.ActivityText.AppendText("Could not remember the last path: $($_.Exception.Message)`r`n") }
    }
    function Start-Work([string]$Operation, [hashtable]$Options, [string]$UndoPath) {
        if ($state.Busy) { return }
        $state.Operation = $Operation
        if ($Options.Path) {
            $selectedItem = Get-Item -LiteralPath $Options.Path
            $state.DisplayRoot = if ($selectedItem.PSIsContainer) { $selectedItem.FullName } else { $selectedItem.DirectoryName }
        }
        $state.InformationIndex = 0
        $state.Control = [hashtable]::Synchronized(@{CancelRequested=$false; Current=''; Completed=0; Total=0; Phase='Starting'; QueueIndex=0; QueueTotal=1})
        $ui.ActivityText.Clear()
        $ui.JournalText.Visibility = 'Collapsed'
        $ui.StatusText.Text = if ($Operation -like '*Preview') { 'Reading the selected files and building a preview…' } else { 'Applying changes. Please keep the app open until it finishes.' }
        Start-Feedback $Operation $Options
        Set-Busy $true
        $worker = [PowerShell]::Create()
        $script = {
            param($Root,$Options,$Operation,$UndoPath,$Control)
            $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
            Import-Module (Join-Path $Root 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
            if ($Operation -like 'Undo*') {
                Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $UndoPath -Preview:($Operation -eq 'UndoPreview')
                return [pscustomobject]@{Undo=$true; Preview=($Operation -eq 'UndoPreview'); Errors=0; Details=@()}
            }
            $paths = @(@($Options.Path) + @($Options.QueuePaths) | Select-Object -Unique)
            $Options.Remove('QueuePaths')
            # Reject overlapping scopes before touching any files. A queue is an explicit set of independent inputs.
            $seen=@{}
            $inputs = @(foreach ($selectedPath in $paths) {
                $selectedItem=Get-Item -LiteralPath $selectedPath -ErrorAction Stop
                if (-not $seen.ContainsKey($selectedItem.FullName)) { $seen[$selectedItem.FullName]=$true; $selectedItem }
            })
            foreach ($input in $inputs) {
                if (-not $input.PSIsContainer -and $input.Extension -ne '.zip') { throw 'Each queued item must be a folder or ZIP.' }
                if (-not $input.PSIsContainer -and ($Options.ExistingOnly -or $Options.OrganizeExisting)) {
                    if ($Options.ExistingOnly) { throw 'Loose-document tasks require folders throughout the queue.' }
                }
                foreach ($other in $inputs) {
                    if ($input -ne $other -and $other.PSIsContainer -and $input.FullName.StartsWith(($other.FullName.TrimEnd('\') + '\'),[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw 'Queue paths overlap. Remove the nested selection and preview again.' }
                }
            }
            $total = [pscustomobject]@{Archives=0; Written=0; Skipped=0; Moved=0; Unclassified=0; Errors=0; Cancelled=$false; Preview=($Operation -eq 'Preview'); Journal=$null; Details=@()}
            $sharedTargets = @{}
            $Control.QueueTotal = $inputs.Count
            foreach ($input in $inputs) {
                if ($Control.CancelRequested) { $total.Cancelled=$true; break }
                $Control.QueueIndex++; $Control.Completed=0; $Control.Total=0
                $jobOptions = @{} + $Options
                $jobOptions.Path=$input.FullName
                if (-not $input.PSIsContainer) { $jobOptions.OrganizeExisting=$false }
                $result = Invoke-LmsUnpacker @jobOptions -Preview ($Operation -eq 'Preview') -Control $Control -PlannedTargets $sharedTargets
                foreach ($field in @('Archives','Written','Skipped','Moved','Unclassified','Errors')) { $total.$field += $result.$field }
                $total.Details += $result.Details
                if ($result.Journal) { $total.Journal = (@($total.Journal,$result.Journal) | Where-Object { $_ }) -join [Environment]::NewLine }
                $total.Cancelled = $result.Cancelled
                if ($result.Errors -gt 0 -or $result.Cancelled) { break }
            }
            return $total
        }
        $null = $worker.AddScript($script).AddArgument($PSScriptRoot).AddArgument($Options).AddArgument($Operation).AddArgument($UndoPath).AddArgument($state.Control)
        $state.Worker = $worker
        try { $state.Handle = $worker.BeginInvoke(); $timer.Start() }
        catch { $worker.Dispose(); $state.Worker = $null; Set-Busy $false; Stop-FeedbackWithError; throw }
    }
    function Read-Activity {
        Update-Feedback
        if ($state.Control -and -not $state.Control.CancelRequested) {
            $progress = $state.Control
            $ui.BusyProgress.IsIndeterminate = $progress.Total -eq 0
            if ($progress.Total -gt 0) { $ui.BusyProgress.Value = [Math]::Min(100,100 * $progress.Completed / $progress.Total) }
            $ui.StatusText.Text = 'Working - you can review the activity log while this runs.'
        }
        $stream = $state.Worker.Streams.Information
        while ($state.InformationIndex -lt $stream.Count) {
            $ui.ActivityText.AppendText([string]$stream[$state.InformationIndex].MessageData + "`r`n")
            $state.InformationIndex++
        }
    }
    $timer = [Windows.Threading.DispatcherTimer]::new()
    $timer.Interval = [TimeSpan]::FromMilliseconds(180)
    $timer.Add_Tick({
        try {
            Read-Activity
            if (-not $state.Handle.IsCompleted) { return }
            $timer.Stop()
            $output = @($state.Worker.EndInvoke($state.Handle))
            Read-Activity
            if ($state.Worker.Streams.Error.Count -gt 0) { throw ($state.Worker.Streams.Error | Out-String) }
            $result = $output | Select-Object -Last 1
            if (-not $result) { throw 'No operation result was returned. Review the activity log.' }
            if ($result.Errors -gt 0) { throw 'Some operations failed. Read Activity and errors for details. Affected ZIPs were kept; some files may already have been written.' }
            if ($state.Operation -like 'Undo*') {
                $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource = $null
                $ui.ResultsTabs.SelectedIndex = 1
                $ui.SummaryText.Text = if ($result.Preview) { 'Undo preview ready. Check the listed original locations before restoring.' } else { 'Original file locations restored. Empty folders are kept.' }
            } else {
                $selectedRoot = $state.DisplayRoot
                $rootPrefix = $selectedRoot.TrimEnd('\') + '\'
                $rows = @($result.Details | ForEach-Object {
                    $destination = if ($_.PSObject.Properties['Target']) { $_.Target } else { $_.Path }
                    $relative = if ($destination -and $destination.StartsWith($rootPrefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { $destination.Substring($rootPrefix.Length) } else { $destination }
                    $comparisonLabel = if ($_.Comparison -like 'Identical*') { 'Identical content' } elseif ($_.Comparison) { 'Different content' } else { '' }
                    [pscustomobject]@{ Action=$_.Action; File=[IO.Path]::GetFileName($_.Source); Source=$_.Source; Course=$_.Course; Destination=$relative; FullDestination=$destination; Reason=$_.Reason; Comparison=$_.Comparison; ComparisonLabel=$comparisonLabel; Bytes=$_.Bytes; Archive=$_.Archive }
                })
                $ui.PlanGrid.ItemsSource = $rows
                $ui.ResultsTabs.SelectedIndex=0
                Update-ReviewFilter
                Update-Hints
                $prefix = if ($result.Preview) { 'Planned' } else { 'Completed' }
                $ui.SummaryText.Text = "$prefix`: $($result.Archives) ZIPs; $($result.Written) extracted; $($result.Moved) organized; $($result.Skipped) skipped; $($result.Unclassified) unclassified."
                if ($result.Journal) { $ui.JournalText.Text = 'Undo record: ' + $result.Journal; $ui.JournalText.Visibility = 'Visible' }
            }
            $state.Preview = if ($result.Preview) { $result } else { $null }
            Set-Busy $false
            $ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled = $result.Preview -and ($state.Operation -eq 'UndoPreview' -or ($result.Archives + $result.Moved -gt 0))
            $ui.ApplyButton.Content = if ($state.Operation -eq 'UndoPreview') { '_Restore original locations' } else { '_Apply changes' }
            $ui.StatusText.Text = if ($ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled) { 'Preview ready • Review destinations and settings, then apply.' } elseif ($result.Preview) { 'Nothing to apply. Check the selected folder, task and scan depth.' } else { 'Finished • Your selected folder is ready to open.' }
            if ($result.PSObject.Properties['Cancelled'] -and $result.Cancelled) {
                $state.Preview=$null; $ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled=$false
                $ui.StatusText.Text='Cancelled safely. Completed work remains; unprocessed ZIPs are kept. Preview again to continue.'
            }
            if (-not $result.Preview -and -not $SmokeTest) { Save-Preferences }
            Complete-Feedback $result
            if (-not $result.Preview) { Request-ZipDiscovery }
        } catch {
            $timer.Stop()
            $state.Preview = $null
            $ui.ActivityText.AppendText("[ERROR] $($_.Exception.Message)`r`n")
            $ui.ResultsTabs.SelectedIndex = 1
            $ui.SummaryText.Text = 'Operation stopped or reported errors. Review the activity log before trying again.'
            Set-Busy $false
            $ui.StatusText.Text = 'Needs attention • See Activity and errors'
            Stop-FeedbackWithError
        } finally {
            if (-not $state.Busy -and $state.Worker) { $state.Worker.Dispose(); $state.Worker = $null; $state.Handle = $null }
        }
    })
    . (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'ProfileEditor.ps1')

    $ui.ApplyButton.Add_Click({

            if (-not [IO.Directory]::Exists($folder)) { throw 'The selected folder does not exist.' }
            Start-Process -FilePath explorer.exe -ArgumentList ('"' + $folder + '"')
        } catch { Show-Problem $_.Exception.Message }
    })

        catch { Show-Problem $_.Exception.Message }
    })

        $event.Handled = $true
    })

        param($sender,$event)
        $event.Handled = $true
        if ($state.Busy -or -not $event.Data.GetDataPresent([Windows.DataFormats]::FileDrop)) { return }
        $paths = @($event.Data.GetData([Windows.DataFormats]::FileDrop))
        Set-SelectedPaths $paths
    })

        if ($state.Busy) { $event.Cancel = $true; $ui.StatusText.Text = 'Please wait for this operation to finish before closing.' }
    })
    $window.Add_Closed({ $timer.Stop(); $discoveryTimer.Stop(); if ($state.DiscoveryWorker) { $state.DiscoveryWorker.Stop(); $state.DiscoveryWorker.Dispose() } })

        if (-not $Path -and $env:LMS_ARGUMENTS) {
            [string[]]$launchArgs = ([Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString([Convert]::FromBase64String($env:LMS_ARGUMENTS)) | ConvertFrom-Json)
            if ($launchArgs.Count -gt 1) { throw 'Open the desktop app with no arguments, or pass one folder or ZIP path.' }
            if ($launchArgs.Count -eq 1) { $Path = $launchArgs[0] }
        }
        if ([IO.File]::Exists($shortcut)) { $ui.RemoveShortcutButton.Visibility = 'Visible' }
    }
    if ($Path -eq '--verify-startup') {
        Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
        Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'DesktopIntegration.psm1') -Force
        $null = Test-LmsProfile (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\nust.json')
        if (-not [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'START-HERE.html'))) { throw 'Embedded guide is missing.' }
        exit 0
    }
    if (-not $SmokeTest) {
        $startup=Get-LmsStartupSelection $Path $state.LaunchFolder $savedPreferences.Path
        $ui.InputPath.Text=$startup.Path
        Request-ZipDiscovery -StartupCandidates $startup.Candidates
    } elseif ($Path) { $ui.InputPath.Text=$Path }
    Update-Hints
    if ($SmokeTest) {
        # Real WPF controls and the asynchronous engine, without showing a window or launching external apps.
        & (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'tests\Test-Desktop.ps1')
    } else {
        $window.Add_ContentRendered({ $null = $ui.FolderButton.Focus() })
        $null = $window.ShowDialog()
    }
} catch {
    if ($SmokeTest) { throw }
    $null = [Windows.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message,'LMS Unpacker could not open','OK','Error')
    exit 1
}

Start-Unpacker.ps1

Guided terminal menu. Selects mode/profile/grouping, previews and asks before applying. Calls the shared engine directly.

Read-Choice

Inputs: prompt, allowed values and default. Repeats until valid, accepts Enter as default and turns Q into cooperative menu cancellation. Used by the guided CLI, not direct option parsing.

Declared at line 6
Read the full source (93 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
[CmdletBinding()]
param([string]$Path)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Read-Choice([string]$Prompt, [string[]]$Allowed, [string]$Default) {
    while ($true) {
        $answer = Read-Host "$Prompt [$Default] (Q to quit)"
        if ($null -eq $answer -or $answer -eq 'q') { throw [OperationCanceledException]::new('Cancelled; no further changes.') }
        if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($answer)) { $answer = $Default }
        if ($answer -in $Allowed) { return $answer }
        Write-Host ('Enter one of: ' + ($Allowed -join ', ') + '.')
    }
}

try {
    Write-Host 'LMS Unpacker'
    Write-Host 'Keyboard guide: type a choice and press Enter. Enter accepts [default]. Q quits.'
    Write-Host 'No administrator access or installation is needed.'
    Write-Host 'New here? Use LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe --tutorial, or open START-HERE.html in the source folder.'
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
    if (-not $Path) {
        $Path = Read-Host 'Folder or ZIP path (paste a path, or press Enter for the current folder)'
        if (-not $Path) { $Path = (Get-Location).Path }
        if ($Path -eq 'q') { return }
    }
    $Path = $Path.Trim().Trim('"')
    $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path
    Write-Host "Selected: $($item.FullName)"
    Write-Host '1. Flatten ZIP downloads (remove nested folders)'
    Write-Host '2. Just unzip (keep folders inside a folder named after each ZIP)'
    Write-Host '3. Organize ZIP downloads and loose course documents'
    Write-Host '4. Organize loose course documents only'
    Write-Host '5. Undo a previous organization'
    $task = Read-Choice 'Task' @('1','2','3','4','5') '1'
    if ($task -eq '5') {
        $report = (Read-Host 'Full path to the undo JSON record').Trim().Trim('"')
        if ($report -eq 'q') { return }
        Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $report -Preview
        if ((Read-Choice 'Restore these files? Y=yes, N=cancel' @('y','n') 'n') -eq 'y') { Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $report }
        return
    }
    $mode = switch ($task) { '1' {'Flatten'} '2' {'Extract'} default {'Organize'} }
    if (-not $item.PSIsContainer -and $task -eq '4') { throw 'Choose a folder to organize existing documents.' }
    $depth = 1
    $conflict = 'Skip'
    $keep = $false
    Write-Host 'Defaults: scan one level of course folders; skip existing names; delete ZIPs after success.'
    if ((Read-Choice 'Settings: 1=use defaults, 2=customize' @('1','2') '1') -eq '2') {
        if ($item.PSIsContainer) { $depth = [int](Read-Choice 'Scan: 0=this folder, 1=include immediate course folders' @('0','1') '1') }
        $conflict = if ((Read-Choice 'Conflicts: 1=skip existing names, 2=rename different versions' @('1','2') '1') -eq '1') { 'Skip' } else { 'Rename' }
        if ($task -ne '4') { $keep = (Read-Choice 'Keep ZIP after success? Y=yes, N=delete (including skipped content)' @('y','n') 'n') -eq 'y' }
    }
    $rules = $null
    $groupBy = 'Category'
    if ($mode -eq 'Organize') {
        Write-Host 'Profiles: 1=general categories; 2=NUST Fall 2026; 3=my JSON profile.'
        Write-Host 'Files without week labels need a matching syllabus profile. General categories cannot infer their teaching week.'
        $profile = Read-Choice 'Profile' @('1','2','3') '1'
        if ($profile -eq '2') { $rules = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'profiles\nust.json' }
        if ($profile -eq '3') {
            $rules = (Read-Host 'Profile JSON path').Trim().Trim('"')
            if ($rules -eq 'q') { return }
            if (-not $rules) { throw 'A profile path is required for option 3.' }
        }
        $groupBy = switch (Read-Choice 'Group lectures: 1=categories only, 2=by week, 3=by topic' @('1','2','3') '1') {
            '2' { 'Week' }
            '3' { 'Topic' }
            default { 'Category' }
        }
        if ($groupBy -eq 'Topic') { Write-Host 'Topic names come from profile rules; unmatched lecture topics are labeled Unsorted topics.' }
    }
    $options = @{
        Path=$item.FullName; Mode=$mode; GroupBy=$groupBy; Depth=$depth; Conflict=$conflict; KeepZip=$keep; RulesPath=$rules
        OrganizeExisting=($task -eq '3' -and $item.PSIsContainer); ExistingOnly=($task -eq '4')
    }
    Write-Host 'Building a preview. Large archives can take a moment to read.'
    $preview = Invoke-LmsUnpacker @options -Preview $true
    if ($preview.Errors -gt 0) { throw 'Preview found errors. Resolve them before applying changes.' }
    if (($preview.Archives + $preview.Moved) -eq 0) { Write-Host 'Nothing to apply.'; return }
    if ((Read-Choice 'Apply the displayed changes? Y=yes, N=cancel' @('y','n') 'n') -eq 'y') {
        $result = Invoke-LmsUnpacker @options
        if ($result.Errors -gt 0) { exit 1 }
    } else { Write-Host 'Cancelled. No changes made.' }
} catch [OperationCanceledException] {
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
} catch {
    Write-Host "[ERROR] $($_.Exception.Message)"
    Write-Host 'Check the path and options, then run Run-Unpack.bat again.'
    Write-Host 'For copy-and-paste launch commands, open START-HERE.html or LAUNCH-HELP.txt.'
    exit 1
}

tests/Run-Tests.ps1

Windows regression scenarios with synthetic ZIPs/documents: integrity, conflicts, hostile paths, grouping, organization, journals and recovery.

#requires -Version 5.1
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
$testRoot = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('.runs\lms-tests-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($testRoot)
$script:passed = 0

function Assert($Condition, [string]$Message) { if (-not $Condition) { throw "ASSERTION: $Message" } }
function New-Case([string]$Name) {
    $path = Join-Path $testRoot $Name
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($path)
    return $path
}
function Write-File([string]$Path, [string]$Text) {
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($Path))
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($Path,$Text)
}
function New-Zip([string]$Path, [System.Collections.IDictionary]$Entries) {
    $zip = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($Path,'Create')
    try {
        foreach ($key in $Entries.Keys) {
            $entry = $zip.CreateEntry($key)
            if (-not $key.EndsWith('/')) {
                $writer = [IO.StreamWriter]::new($entry.Open())
                try { $writer.Write([string]$Entries[$key]) } finally { $writer.Dispose() }
            }
        }
    } finally { $zip.Dispose() }
}
function Test-Case([string]$Name, [scriptblock]$Body) {
    & $Body
    $script:passed++
    Write-Host "PASS: $Name"
}

try {
    Test-Case 'Default flattens, skips different existing content, deletes ZIP' {
        $dir = New-Case 'defaults'
        Write-File "$dir\a.txt" 'original'
        New-Zip "$dir\download.zip" ([ordered]@{'nested/a.txt'='new'; 'nested/b.txt'='second'})
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and $r.Skipped -eq 1 -and $r.Written -eq 1) 'default counts'
        Assert ([IO.File]::ReadAllText("$dir\a.txt") -eq 'original') 'existing content changed'
        Assert ([IO.File]::ReadAllText("$dir\b.txt") -eq 'second') 'flattened file missing'
        Assert (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath "$dir\download.zip")) 'ZIP not deleted'
    }
    Test-Case 'Preview creates nothing and models collisions across entries and archives' {
        $dir = New-Case 'preview [literal]'
        New-Zip "$dir\a.zip" ([ordered]@{'one/a.txt'='one'; 'two/a.txt'='two'})
        New-Zip "$dir\b.zip" @{'a.txt'='three'}
        $before = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dir -Recurse | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName + (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName).Hash })
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -Preview $true
        $after = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dir -Recurse | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName + (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $_.FullName).Hash })
        Assert (($before -join '|') -eq ($after -join '|')) 'preview mutated inputs'
        Assert ($r.Written -eq 1 -and $r.Skipped -eq 2 -and $r.Errors -eq 0) 'preview collision accounting'
    }
    Test-Case 'Rename is repeatable and recognizes an already renamed version' {
        $dir = New-Case 'rename'
        Write-File "$dir\a.txt" 'old'
        New-Zip "$dir\a.zip" @{'nested/a.txt'='new'}
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -Conflict Rename -KeepZip $true
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and (Test-Path "$dir\a_1.txt")) 'rename failed'
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -Conflict Rename -KeepZip $true
        Assert ($r.Written -eq 0 -and $r.Skipped -eq 1) 'rerun generated another copy'
        Assert (-not (Test-Path "$dir\a_2.txt")) 'unnecessary second version'
    }
    Test-Case 'Explicit overwrite replaces content' {
        $dir = New-Case 'overwrite'
        Write-File "$dir\a.txt" 'old'
        New-Zip "$dir\a.zip" @{'a.txt'='new'}
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -Conflict Overwrite
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 0) 'overwrite error'
        Assert ([IO.File]::ReadAllText("$dir\a.txt") -eq 'new') 'overwrite not applied'
    }
    Test-Case 'Ordinary extraction preserves structure, empty folders and literal filenames' {
        $dir = New-Case 'extract'
        New-Zip "$dir\bundle.zip" ([ordered]@{'one/a[1].txt'='one'; 'two/a[1].txt'='two'; 'empty/'=''})
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path "$dir\bundle.zip" -Mode Extract
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and $r.Written -eq 2) 'extract failed'
        Assert (Test-Path -LiteralPath "$dir\bundle\one\a[1].txt") 'literal file missing'
        Assert (Test-Path -LiteralPath "$dir\bundle\empty" -PathType Container) 'empty folder missing'
    }
    Test-Case 'Traversal, absolute, ADS and reserved paths rejected before writes' {
        foreach ($bad in @('../escape.txt','/absolute.txt','C:/root.txt','safe/CON.txt','safe/a.txt:stream','safe/../escape.txt','safe/trailing.')) {
            $dir = New-Case ([guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
            New-Zip "$dir\bad.zip" ([ordered]@{'good.txt'='good'; $bad='bad'})
            $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir
            Assert ($r.Errors -eq 1) "unsafe path accepted: $bad"
            Assert ((Test-Path "$dir\bad.zip") -and -not (Test-Path "$dir\good.txt")) 'unsafe archive modified output'
        }
    }
    Test-Case 'Broken ZIP retained while another ZIP completes' {
        $dir = New-Case 'broken'
        Write-File "$dir\a.zip" 'not an archive'
        New-Zip "$dir\b.zip" @{'good.txt'='yes'}
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 1 -and $r.Archives -eq 1) 'failure did not isolate archive'
        Assert (Test-Path "$dir\a.zip") 'broken ZIP deleted'
    }
    Test-Case 'Entry and expanded byte limits retain source' {
        $dir = New-Case 'limits'
        New-Zip "$dir\a.zip" @{'a.txt'='12345'; 'b.txt'='67890'}
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -MaxEntries 1
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 1) 'entry limit ignored'
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir -MaxExpandedBytes 5
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 1 -and (Test-Path "$dir\a.zip")) 'size limit ignored'
    }
    Test-Case 'File-folder conflicts reject archive before publishing files' {

        $dir = New-Case 'organize'

        Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $r.Journal
        Assert (Test-Path "$dir\Biology\Assignment 1.pdf") 'undo missed assignment'
        Assert (Test-Path "$dir\Biology\cell structure.pdf") 'undo missed lecture'

    Test-Case 'CRC corruption detected even when content can be decompressed' {
        $dir = New-Case 'crc'
        New-Zip "$dir\bad.zip" @{'lecture.pdf'='valid compressed payload'}
        $bytes = [IO.File]::ReadAllBytes("$dir\bad.zip")
        for ($i=0; $i -lt $bytes.Length-20; $i++) {
            if ([BitConverter]::ToUInt32($bytes,$i) -eq 0x02014b50) {
                $bytes[$i+16] = $bytes[$i+16] -bxor 1
                break
            }
        }
        [IO.File]::WriteAllBytes("$dir\bad.zip",$bytes)
        $r = Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $dir
        Assert ($r.Errors -eq 1 -and (Test-Path "$dir\bad.zip")) 'CRC failure lost archive'
        Assert (-not (Test-Path "$dir\lecture.pdf")) 'corrupt entry published'

    }
}

tests/Test-Cleanup.ps1

Runs the cleaner in an isolated fake repository with synthetic artifacts. Verifies preview makes no changes, incomplete releases block removal and current/newer/unknown files survive.

Assert-Cleanup

Cleanup-test assertion. Checks observable preservation/deletion outcomes in a synthetic repository, never in real course materials.

Declared at line 5
Read the full source (40 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$repo=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
$fixture=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('.runs/cleanup-'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
function Assert-Cleanup($Condition,[string]$Message) { if (-not $Condition) { throw "Cleanup check failed: $Message" } }
try {
    foreach($folder in @('tools','dist','tests/.runs')) { $null=[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory((Join-Path $fixture $folder)) }
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath (Join-Path $repo 'tools/Clean-Workspace.ps1') -Destination (Join-Path $fixture 'tools/Clean-Workspace.ps1')
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $fixture 'VERSION'),'2.0.0')
    $current=@('LMS-Unpacker.exe','LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe','LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh','LMS-Unpacker-2.0.0.zip')
    foreach($name in $current) {
        $file=Join-Path $fixture ('dist/'+$name)
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($file,'Synthetic artifact; never executed')
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText(($file+'.sha256'),(Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $file -Algorithm SHA256).Hash+'  '+$name)
    }
    foreach($name in @('dist/LMS-Unpacker-1.0.0.zip','dist/LMS-Unpacker-1.0.0.zip.sha256','dist/LMS-Unpacker-3.0.0.zip','dist/unknown.txt','personal.txt','tests/.runs/fixture.txt')) {
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $fixture $name),'fixture')
    }
    $cleaner=Join-Path $fixture 'tools/Clean-Workspace.ps1'
    & $cleaner
    Assert-Cleanup ([IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture 'dist/LMS-Unpacker-1.0.0.zip'))) 'preview deleted an old package'
    # An incomplete current release must prevent all deletion, even scratch.
    $sidecar=Join-Path $fixture 'dist/LMS-Unpacker-2.0.0.zip.sha256'
    $checksum=[IO.File]::ReadAllText($sidecar); [IO.File]::Delete($sidecar)
    $rejected=$false; try { & $cleaner -Apply } catch { $rejected=$true }
    Assert-Cleanup ($rejected -and [IO.Directory]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture 'tests/.runs'))) 'incomplete current release must block deletion'
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($sidecar,$checksum)
    & $cleaner -Apply
    Assert-Cleanup (-not [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture 'dist/LMS-Unpacker-1.0.0.zip')) -and -not [IO.Directory]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture 'tests/.runs'))) 'known old package and scratch were not removed'
    foreach($name in $current) { Assert-Cleanup ([IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture ('dist/'+$name)))) ('current artifact lost: '+$name) }
    foreach($name in @('dist/LMS-Unpacker-3.0.0.zip','dist/unknown.txt','personal.txt')) { Assert-Cleanup ([IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $fixture $name))) ('unrecognized/newer file lost: '+$name) }
    Write-Host 'PASS: Cleanup preview, incomplete-release refusal, current/newer/unknown-file preservation and bounded removal'
} finally {
    $resolved=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($fixture)
    $prefix=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.runs')).TrimEnd('\','/')+[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
    if ($resolved.StartsWith($prefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -and [IO.Path]::GetFileName($resolved) -like 'cleanup-*' -and [IO.Directory]::Exists($resolved)) {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolved -Recurse -Force
    }
}

tests/Test-Desktop.ps1

Runs inside the real desktop UI scope with SmokeTest. Exercises buttons, background workers, picker setup, queue, dialogs, progress, discovery, popup wrapping and minimum-size layout. Optional WPF renders are test artifacts.

Check-Desktop

Desktop test assertion: throws a labeled failure when an observable UI condition is false.

Declared at line 5

Click-Control

Desktop test helper: raises a real WPF Button.Click routed event on a named control, exercising its actual handler without mouse automation.

Declared at line 6

Wait-Desktop

Desktop test helper: pumps dispatcher frames until background work finishes or a deadline expires. Allows real timer/runspace integration to execute.

Declared at line 7

Wait-Discovery

Desktop test helper: pumps dispatcher frames until discovery worker/pending requests settle, with a bounded timeout.

Declared at line 20

Make-DesktopZip

Creates a synthetic ZIP entry/content for desktop flow tests and reliably disposes archive/writer resources.

Declared at line 30
Read the full source (286 lines)
# Called by Start-Desktop.ps1 -SmokeTest in its UI context. No real user folders or shortcuts are changed.
$fixtureRoot = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('.runs\desktop-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($fixtureRoot)
$desktopPassed = 0
function Check-Desktop($Condition,[string]$Message) { if (-not $Condition) { throw "Desktop check failed: $Message" } }
function Click-Control([string]$Name) { $ui[$Name].RaiseEvent([Windows.RoutedEventArgs]::new([Windows.Controls.Button]::ClickEvent)) }
function Wait-Desktop {
    $deadline = [DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(45)
    while ($state.Busy -and [DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline) {
        $frame = [Windows.Threading.DispatcherFrame]::new()
        $pulse = [Windows.Threading.DispatcherTimer]::new()
        $pulse.Interval = [TimeSpan]::FromMilliseconds(20)
        $pulse.Tag = $frame
        $pulse.Add_Tick({ param($sender,$event); $sender.Stop(); $sender.Tag.Continue = $false })
        $pulse.Start()
        [Windows.Threading.Dispatcher]::PushFrame($frame)
    }
    Check-Desktop (-not $state.Busy) 'background operation timed out'
}
function Wait-Discovery {
    $deadline=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(30)
    while (($state.DiscoveryWorker -or $state.DiscoveryPending -or $discoveryTimer.IsEnabled) -and [DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline) {
        $frame=[Windows.Threading.DispatcherFrame]::new()
        $pulse=[Windows.Threading.DispatcherTimer]::new(); $pulse.Interval=[TimeSpan]::FromMilliseconds(30); $pulse.Tag=$frame
        $pulse.Add_Tick({ param($sender,$event); $sender.Stop(); $sender.Tag.Continue=$false }); $pulse.Start()
        [Windows.Threading.Dispatcher]::PushFrame($frame)
    }
    Check-Desktop (-not $state.DiscoveryWorker -and -not $state.DiscoveryPending) 'discovery timed out'
}
function Make-DesktopZip([string]$ZipPath,[string]$Entry,[string]$Content) {
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    $archive = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($ZipPath,'Create')
    try { $writer = [IO.StreamWriter]::new($archive.CreateEntry($Entry).Open()); try { $writer.Write($Content) } finally { $writer.Dispose() } }
    finally { $archive.Dispose() }
}
try {
    $ui.InputPath.Text = $fixtureRoot
    Check-Desktop (-not $ui.ApplyButton.IsEnabled -and -not $ui.KeepZip.IsChecked -and $ui.ConflictChoice.SelectedIndex -eq 0) 'initial defaults'
    Make-DesktopZip (Join-Path $fixtureRoot 'download.zip') 'section/Lecture 1.pdf' 'fixture'
    Click-Control PreviewButton
    Check-Desktop ($state.Busy -and $ui.FeedbackTitle.Text -eq 'Building your preview...' -and $ui.BusyProgress.IsIndeterminate -and $window.TaskbarItemInfo.ProgressState -eq 'Indeterminate') 'immediate preview feedback before worker finishes'
    Wait-Desktop

    if ($RenderPath) {
        $bitmap=[Windows.Media.Imaging.RenderTargetBitmap]::new([int][Math]::Ceiling($tip.ActualWidth),[int][Math]::Ceiling($tip.ActualHeight),96,96,[Windows.Media.PixelFormats]::Pbgra32)

    $window.Content.Arrange([Windows.Rect]::new(0,0,760,480)); $window.Content.UpdateLayout()

    Write-Host 'PASS: Wrapped popup content, long paths, separate settings tabs and compact-window layout'; $desktopPassed++
    Write-Host "All $desktopPassed desktop flow tests passed."
} finally {
    if ($state.Busy -and $state.Worker) { $state.Worker.Stop(); $state.Worker.Dispose(); $state.Busy = $false }
    $timer.Stop()
    $discoveryTimer.Stop()
    if ($state.DiscoveryWorker) { $state.DiscoveryWorker.Stop(); $state.DiscoveryWorker.Dispose(); $state.DiscoveryWorker=$null }
    $resolvedFixture = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($fixtureRoot)
    $safePrefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.runs')).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
    if ($resolvedFixture.StartsWith($safePrefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -and [IO.Path]::GetFileName($resolvedFixture) -like 'desktop-*') {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolvedFixture -Recurse -Force
    }
}

tests/Test-Guide.js

Node built-in tests for offline guide structure and interactions using a lightweight DOM stub. Includes handbook/reference checks; no browser rendering claim.

checkOfflinePage

Guide-test structural check for unique IDs, valid internal anchors and absent remote assets in handbook/reference pages.

Declared at line 6

node

Guide-test DOM stub factory. Supplies text/value/attributes, event registration and async event firing sufficient for the offline example scripts. Not a browser layout engine.

// Offline guide behavior checks using Node's built-ins; no browser or network required.
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const vm = require('node:vm');
function checkOfflinePage(relative) {
  const filename = path.join(__dirname, '..', relative);
  const page = fs.readFileSync(filename, 'utf8');
  const pageIds = [...page.matchAll(/\bid="([^"]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
  assert.equal(new Set(pageIds).size, pageIds.length, `${relative}: duplicate IDs`);
  for (const match of page.matchAll(/href="([^"]+)"/g)) {
    const href = match[1];
    if (href.startsWith('#')) assert(pageIds.includes(href.slice(1)), `${relative}: missing anchor ${href}`);
    else if (!/^[a-z]+:/i.test(href)) assert(fs.existsSync(path.resolve(path.dirname(filename), href)), `${relative}: missing local link ${href}`);
  }
  assert(!/<(?:script|link|img|iframe)\b[^>]*(?:src|href)=["']https?:/i.test(page), `${relative}: remote asset`);
  return page;
}
const html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'START-HERE.html'), 'utf8');
const ids = [...html.matchAll(/\bid="([^"]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1]);
assert.equal(new Set(ids).size, ids.length, 'HTML IDs must be unique');
for (const match of html.matchAll(/href="#([^"]+)"/g)) assert(ids.includes(match[1]), `Missing anchor ${match[1]}`);
assert(!/<(?:script|link|img|iframe)\b[^>]*(?:src|href)=["']https?:/i.test(html), 'Guide must work without remote assets');
const nodes = new Map();
function node(attributes = {}, text = '') {
  return {dataset: {}, textContent: text, value: '', checked: false, hidden: false, disabled: false, attributes,
    events: {}, addEventListener(event, callback) { this.events[event] = callback; },
    setAttribute(key, value) { this.attributes[key] = value; }, removeAttribute(key) { delete this.attributes[key]; }, focus() {},
    async fire(event) { if (this.events[event]) await this.events[event](); }};
}
for (const id of ids) nodes.set(id, node());
nodes.get('demo-depth').value = '1';
nodes.get('group-by').value = 'Category';
nodes.get('calculus-group').value = 'Week';
const modes = ['Flatten', 'Extract', 'Organize'].map(mode => Object.assign(node(), {dataset: {mode}}));
const steps = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(step => Object.assign(node(), {dataset: {step: String(step)}}));
const topics = [...html.matchAll(/<details data-help-topic>([\s\S]*?)<\/details>/g)].map(m => node({}, m[1].replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ')));
const document = {
  getElementById(id) { assert(nodes.has(id), `Unknown element ${id}`); return nodes.get(id); },
  querySelectorAll(selector) { return {'[data-mode]': modes, '[data-step]': steps, '[data-help-topic]': topics}[selector] || []; },
  createRange() { return {selectNodeContents() {}}; }};
const context = {document, navigator: {}, window: {getSelection() { return {removeAllRanges() {}, addRange() {}}; }}};
const script = [...html.matchAll(/<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)].map(m => m[1]).join('\n');
vm.runInNewContext(script, context, {timeout: 1000});
(async () => {
  assert.match(nodes.get('demo-scan-status').textContent, /^2 ZIPs/);
  nodes.get('demo-depth').value = '0'; await nodes.get('demo-depth').fire('change');
  assert.equal(nodes.get('demo-found').textContent, 'general.zip');
  nodes.get('demo-depth').value = '1'; await nodes.get('demo-depth').fire('change');
  assert.match(nodes.get('demo-found').textContent, /Calculus\/lecture.zip/);
  nodes.get('help-search').value = 'workspace'; await nodes.get('help-search').fire('input');
  assert(topics.some(topic => !topic.hidden) && topics.some(topic => topic.hidden));
  nodes.get('help-search').value = 'no-such-topic-493'; await nodes.get('help-search').fire('input');
  assert(topics.every(topic => topic.hidden)); assert.match(nodes.get('help-search-status').textContent, /No topics match/);
  nodes.get('help-search').value = ''; await nodes.get('help-search').fire('input');
  assert(topics.every(topic => !topic.hidden));
  for (const mode of modes) {
    await mode.fire('click');
    for (const group of ['Category', 'Week', 'Topic']) {
      nodes.get('group-by').value = group; await nodes.get('group-by').fire('change');
      for (const keep of [false, true]) {
        nodes.get('keep-zip').checked = keep; await nodes.get('keep-zip').fire('change');
        assert.equal(nodes.get('zip-label').textContent, keep ? 'ZIP KEPT' : 'ZIP DELETED');
        assert(nodes.get('result-tree').textContent.includes('Quantitative/'));
      }
    }
  }
  for (const step of steps) { await step.fire('click'); assert.match(nodes.get('step-count').textContent, /^Step [1-5] of 5$/); }
  nodes.get('calculus-group').value = 'Topic'; await nodes.get('calculus-group').fire('change');
  assert.match(nodes.get('calculus-tree').textContent, /Limits and continuity/);
  await nodes.get('copy-launch').fire('click'); assert.match(nodes.get('launch-status').textContent, /Ctrl\+C/);
  await nodes.get('copy-command').fire('click'); assert.match(nodes.get('copy-status').textContent, /Ctrl\+C/);
  console.log('PASS: Offline HTML, anchors, scan depth, searchable help, all modes/groupings, CLI steps, Calculus and clipboard fallback');
  const handbook = checkOfflinePage('docs/BUILD-IT-YOURSELF.html');
  const demo = new Map(['policy','same','keep','conflict-result'].map(id => [id,node()]));
  demo.get('policy').value='Skip'; demo.get('same').value='different'; demo.get('keep').value='delete';
  const handbookScript = [...handbook.matchAll(/<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)].map(m=>m[1]).join('\n');
  vm.runInNewContext(handbookScript,{document:{getElementById(id){assert(demo.has(id));return demo.get(id);}}},{timeout:1000});
  for (const policy of ['Skip','Rename','Overwrite']) for (const same of ['same','different']) for (const keep of ['keep','delete']) {
    demo.get('policy').value=policy; demo.get('same').value=same; demo.get('keep').value=keep;
    await demo.get('policy').fire('change');
    const result=demo.get('conflict-result').textContent;
    assert(result.includes(same==='same'?'Duplicate':policy==='Rename'?'lecture_1.pdf':policy));
    assert(result.includes(keep==='keep'?'Source ZIP: kept':'Source ZIP: permanently deleted'));
  }
  const reference=checkOfflinePage('docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html');
  assert(reference.includes('Invoke-LmsArchive') && reference.includes('Desktop.xaml'));
  const entries=[...reference.matchAll(/<article data-entry[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/article>/g)].map(m=>node({},m[1]));
  const search=node(), count=node();
  vm.runInNewContext([...reference.matchAll(/<script>([\s\S]*?)<\/script>/g)].map(m=>m[1]).join('\n'),{document:{querySelectorAll(){return entries;},getElementById(id){return id==='search'?search:count;}}},{timeout:1000});
  search.value='Copy-LmsEntry'; await search.fire('input');
  assert(entries.some(entry=>!entry.hidden) && entries.some(entry=>entry.hidden));
  // The reference embeds this test's source, so a fixed test phrase would match itself.
  search.value='unfindable '+Date.now()+' '+Math.random(); await search.fire('input'); assert(entries.every(entry=>entry.hidden));
  assert.match(count.textContent,/No matches/);
  search.value=''; await search.fire('input'); assert(entries.every(entry=>!entry.hidden));
  console.log('PASS: Handbook links, twelve collision/retention scenarios, complete reference and searchable source');
})().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

tests/Test-Linux.sh

End-to-end generated Linux shell checks from an independent folder, including paths with spaces, dry-run/apply and error exit behavior.

No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.

Read the full source (27 lines)
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
repo="$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)"
fixture="$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/lms-shell-test.XXXXXXXX")"
trap 'rm -rf -- "$fixture"' EXIT
mkdir "$fixture/standalone & spaces"
cp "$repo/dist/LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh" "$fixture/standalone & spaces/unpacker.sh"
app="$fixture/standalone & spaces/unpacker.sh"
bash -n "$app"
bash "$app" --help
export LMS_TEST_ROOT="$fixture"
pwsh -NoProfile -Command '
  $zip=[IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open((Join-Path $env:LMS_TEST_ROOT "sample.zip"),"Create")
  try { $writer=[IO.StreamWriter]::new($zip.CreateEntry("nested/hello.txt").Open()); $writer.Write("hello"); $writer.Dispose() } finally { $zip.Dispose() }
'
bash "$app" -Path "$fixture/sample.zip" -OutputPath "$fixture/output" -Mode Extract -DryRun
test -f "$fixture/sample.zip"
test ! -e "$fixture/output"
bash "$app" -Path "$fixture/sample.zip" -OutputPath "$fixture/output" -Mode Extract
test ! -f "$fixture/sample.zip"
test "$(cat "$fixture/output/sample/nested/hello.txt")" = hello
if bash "$app" -Path "$fixture/missing.zip"; then
  printf '%s\n' 'Missing input incorrectly returned success' >&2
  exit 1
fi
printf '%s\n' 'PASS: Independent Linux launcher, quoted paths, preview, extraction and error status'

tests/Test-Portable.ps1

Portable engine/profile regression fixtures that run under both Windows and Linux PowerShell.

Assert-Portable

Portable-suite assertion that works without Windows-only testing libraries.

Declared at line 7

Make-PortableZip

Creates synthetic ZIP entries for cross-platform engine tests using .NET compression APIs.

Declared at line 8
Read the full source (97 lines)
# Cross-platform integration tests: only synthetic files, no user materials.
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '../LmsUnpacker.psm1') -Force
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '../ProfileEditor.ps1')
$run=Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('.runs/portable-'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$null=[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($run)
function Assert-Portable($Condition,[string]$Message) { if (-not $Condition) { throw $Message } }
function Make-PortableZip([string]$Path,$Entries) {
    $zip=[IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($Path,'Create')
    try { foreach ($key in $Entries.Keys) {
        $writer=[IO.StreamWriter]::new($zip.CreateEntry($key).Open())
        try { $writer.Write([string]$Entries[$key]) } finally { $writer.Dispose() }
    } } finally { $zip.Dispose() }
}
try {
    $inputFolder=Join-Path $run 'input with spaces & punctuation'
    $outputFolder=Join-Path $run 'output'
    $null=[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($inputFolder)
    $source=Join-Path $inputFolder 'sample.zip'
    Make-PortableZip $source @{'nested/note.txt'='first'}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $source -OutputPath $outputFolder -Preview $true
    Assert-Portable ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and -not [IO.Directory]::Exists($outputFolder) -and [IO.File]::Exists($source)) 'Output preview must not create directories or delete sources'
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $source -OutputPath $outputFolder
    Assert-Portable ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and [IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $outputFolder 'note.txt')) -eq 'first' -and -not [IO.File]::Exists($source)) 'Output extraction and delete defaults'
    Make-PortableZip $source @{'nested/note.txt'='different'}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $source -OutputPath $outputFolder
    Assert-Portable ($r.Skipped -eq 1 -and -not [IO.File]::Exists($source) -and [IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $outputFolder 'note.txt')) -eq 'first') 'Skip and delete defaults at external destination'
    Assert-Portable ($r.Details[0].Comparison -like 'Different content*existing file*' -and $r.Details[0].Bytes -eq 9) 'Different existing content must be explicit'
    Make-PortableZip $source @{'nested/note.txt'='first'}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $source -OutputPath $outputFolder -Preview $true
    Assert-Portable ($r.Details[0].Action -eq 'Duplicate' -and $r.Details[0].Comparison -like 'Identical content*existing file*') 'Identical content comparison'
    [IO.File]::Delete($source)
    Make-PortableZip $source @{'nested/note.txt'='new version'}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $source -OutputPath $outputFolder -Preview $true -Conflict Rename
    Assert-Portable ($r.Details[0].Target.EndsWith('note_1.txt') -and $r.Details[0].Comparison -like 'Different content*') 'Renamed files must retain conflict explanation'
    Write-Host 'PASS: External ZIP destination, preview and defaults'

    $a=Join-Path $inputFolder 'a.zip'; $b=Join-Path $inputFolder 'b.zip'
    Make-PortableZip $a @{'same.txt'='a'}; Make-PortableZip $b @{'same.txt'='b'}
    $planned=@{}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $a -OutputPath $outputFolder -Preview $true -PlannedTargets $planned
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $b -OutputPath $outputFolder -Preview $true -PlannedTargets $planned
    Assert-Portable ($r.Skipped -eq 1 -and $r.Written -eq 0) 'Queue preview must detect collisions across selected paths'
    $control=[hashtable]::Synchronized(@{CancelRequested=$true; Current=''; Completed=0; Total=0; Phase=''})
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $a -Control $control
    Assert-Portable ($r.Cancelled -and $r.Archives -eq 0 -and [IO.File]::Exists($a)) 'Cancelled work must retain unprocessed ZIPs'
    Write-Host 'PASS: Shared queue collision plan and cancellation'

    $profile=Join-Path $run 'profile.json'
    Save-VisualProfile $profile @([pscustomobject]@{Code='BIO-101'; Folder='Biology'; Name='Biology'}) @([pscustomobject]@{Course='BIO-101'; Pattern='*cell*'; Folder='Lectures/Week 02'; Topic='Cell biology'; Reason='Local syllabus'})
    $courseZip=Join-Path $inputFolder 'BIO-101-course.zip'
    Make-PortableZip $courseZip @{'section/cell.pdf'='fixture'}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $courseZip -Mode Organize -GroupBy Topic -RulesPath $profile -OutputPath $outputFolder
    Assert-Portable ($r.Errors -eq 0 -and [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $outputFolder 'Biology/Lectures/Cell biology/cell.pdf'))) 'Profile editor output must route any course by topic'
    $rejected=$false
    try { Save-VisualProfile (Join-Path $run 'bad.json') @() @([pscustomobject]@{Course='*'; Pattern='*'; Folder='../escape'}) } catch { $rejected=$true }
    Assert-Portable ($rejected -and -not [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $run 'bad.json'))) 'Profile validation must reject unsafe rules before save'
    Write-Host 'PASS: Visual profile model, generic course routing, safe validation'

    $loose=Join-Path $run 'Biology'; $null=[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($loose)
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $loose 'cell.pdf'),'fixture')
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $loose 'mystery.pdf'),'unknown')
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $loose -Mode Organize -ExistingOnly $true -GroupBy Week -RulesPath $profile
    Assert-Portable ($r.Moved -eq 1 -and $r.Unclassified -eq 1 -and $r.Errors -eq 0) 'Loose file organization and unclassified review'
    $unknown=@($r.Details | Where-Object Action -eq 'Unclassified')[0]
    Assert-Portable ($unknown.Course -eq 'BIO-101' -and $unknown.Source.EndsWith('mystery.pdf')) 'Review needs original course and filename'
    Undo-LmsOrganization -ReportPath $r.Journal
    Assert-Portable ([IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $loose 'cell.pdf'))) 'Undo must restore organized files'
    Write-Host 'PASS: Cross-platform organization, review metadata and undo'

    foreach ($bad in @('../outside.txt','/absolute.txt','safe/CON.txt','a.txt:stream')) {
        $malformed=Join-Path $run ([guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')+'.zip')
        Make-PortableZip $malformed @{$bad='invalid'}
        $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $malformed -OutputPath $outputFolder
        Assert-Portable ($r.Errors -eq 1 -and [IO.File]::Exists($malformed)) 'Unsafe ZIP must be retained and rejected'
    }
    Write-Host 'PASS: Cross-platform unsafe ZIP rejection'
    $limited=Join-Path $run 'limited.zip'
    Make-PortableZip $limited @{'large.txt'=('x'*2048)}
    $r=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $limited -MaxExpandedBytes 128
    Assert-Portable ($r.Errors -eq 1 -and [IO.File]::Exists($limited)) 'Expanded-size limit must retain source'
    if ($env:OS -ne 'Windows_NT') {
        $link=Join-Path $run 'linked-output'
        $null=New-Item -ItemType SymbolicLink -Path $link -Target $outputFolder
        $rejected=$false
        try { $null=Invoke-LmsUnpacker -Path $limited -OutputPath $link } catch { $rejected=$true }
        Assert-Portable ($rejected -and [IO.File]::Exists($limited)) 'Linux output symlinks must be rejected'
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $link
    }
    Write-Host 'PASS: Expanded-size limit and platform link protection'
    Write-Host 'All portable integration checks passed.'
} finally {
    $resolved=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($run)
    $prefix=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.runs')).TrimEnd([IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar)+[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
    if ($resolved.StartsWith($prefix,[StringComparison]::Ordinal) -and [IO.Path]::GetFileName($resolved).StartsWith('portable-')) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolved -Recurse -Force }
}

tests/Test-Release.ps1

Checks packaged desktop startup, CLI argument parsing/extraction, isolated installed-copy startup and runtime cleanup using the actual built EXEs.

Assert-Release

Packaged-release assertion with a labeled error. Used to fail builds when actual EXE behavior differs from the contract.

Declared at line 6

Invoke-DesktopCheck

Launches the packaged desktop startup-verification mode as a hidden process, waits with a timeout and requires a successful exit. No normal interactive app window is requested.

Declared at line 7
Read the full source (68 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$CliPath,[Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$DesktopPath)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$releaseRoot = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot ('.runs\release-' + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
$null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($releaseRoot)
function Assert-Release($Condition,[string]$Message) { if (-not $Condition) { throw "Release test failed: $Message" } }
function Invoke-DesktopCheck([string]$Executable) {
    $start = [Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo]::new($Executable,'--verify-startup')
    $start.UseShellExecute = $false; $start.CreateNoWindow = $true; $start.WindowStyle = 'Hidden'
    $process = [Diagnostics.Process]::Start($start)
    try {
        if (-not $process.WaitForExit(30000)) { $process.Kill(); throw 'Packaged desktop startup timed out.' }
        Assert-Release ($process.ExitCode -eq 0) 'packaged desktop could not load its UI, engine and profile'
    } finally { $process.Dispose() }
}
try {
    $onlyFiles = Join-Path $releaseRoot 'Only these files & spaces'
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($onlyFiles)
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $CliPath -Destination $onlyFiles
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $DesktopPath -Destination $onlyFiles
    $cli = Join-Path $onlyFiles 'LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe'
    $desktop = Join-Path $onlyFiles 'LMS-Unpacker.exe'
    $tempBefore = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) -Directory -Filter 'lms-unpacker-*' | Select-Object -ExpandProperty FullName)
    $help = & $cli --help
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and ($help -join ' ') -match 'DELETE ZIPs') 'CLI help or embedded runtime failed'
    @('q') | & $cli
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) 'standalone guided CLI menu failed'
    $materials = Join-Path $releaseRoot 'materials & [brackets]'
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($materials)
    $archivePath = Join-Path $materials 'example.zip'
    Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
    $archive = [IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($archivePath,'Create')
    try { $writer = [IO.StreamWriter]::new($archive.CreateEntry('nested/example.txt').Open()); try { $writer.Write('fixture') } finally { $writer.Dispose() } } finally { $archive.Dispose() }
    & $cli -Path $archivePath -Mode Extract -DryRun
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and [IO.File]::Exists($archivePath) -and -not [IO.Directory]::Exists((Join-Path $materials 'example'))) 'CLI preview changed files or failed'
    & $cli -Path $archivePath -Mode Extract
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and -not [IO.File]::Exists($archivePath) -and [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $materials 'example\nested\example.txt'))) 'standalone extraction failed'
    $failure = & $cli -Path (Join-Path $materials 'missing.zip') -DryRun
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 1 -and ($failure -join ' ') -match 'does not exist') 'CLI did not propagate an understandable failure'
    $calculus = Join-Path $materials 'Calculus'
    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($calculus)
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $calculus 'Continuity.pdf'),'fixture')
    & $cli -Path $calculus -Mode Organize -ExistingOnly -GroupBy Topic -RulesPath NUST -DryRun
    Assert-Release ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0 -and [IO.File]::Exists((Join-Path $calculus 'Continuity.pdf'))) 'embedded NUST profile or topic preview failed'
    Invoke-DesktopCheck $desktop
    Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..\DesktopIntegration.psm1') -Force
    $shortcutFile = Join-Path $releaseRoot 'LMS Unpacker.lnk'
    $installed = Add-LmsStartShortcut -Executable $desktop -InstallFolder (Join-Path $releaseRoot 'Installed app') -ShortcutPath $shortcutFile
    $shell = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
    try {
        $link = $shell.CreateShortcut($shortcutFile)
        try { Assert-Release ($link.TargetPath -eq $installed -and [IO.File]::Exists($installed)) 'shortcut target does not point to the copied app' }
        finally { $null = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($link) }
    } finally { $null = [Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::FinalReleaseComObject($shell) }
    Invoke-DesktopCheck $installed
    $null = Add-LmsStartShortcut -Executable $installed -InstallFolder ([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($installed)) -ShortcutPath $shortcutFile
    $null = Add-LmsStartShortcut -Executable $desktop -InstallFolder ([IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($installed)) -ShortcutPath $shortcutFile
    $tempAfter = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) -Directory -Filter 'lms-unpacker-*' | Where-Object { $_.FullName -notin $tempBefore })
    Assert-Release ($tempAfter.Count -eq 0) 'portable runtime did not clean up its temporary directory'
    Write-Host 'PASS: Both standalone executables, CLI preview/apply/errors, copied Start menu target, repeat shortcut installation and temporary cleanup'
} finally {
    $resolvedRelease = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath($releaseRoot)
    $testPrefix = [IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '.runs')).TrimEnd('\') + '\'
    if ($resolvedRelease.StartsWith($testPrefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase) -and [IO.Path]::GetFileName($resolvedRelease) -like 'release-*') {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $resolvedRelease -Recurse -Force
    }
}

function ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml([string]$Text) { [Net.WebUtility]::HtmlEncode($Text) }
function Get-ReferenceId([string]$Path) { 'file-' + ($Path -replace '[^A-Za-z0-9_-]','-') }
Push-Location $root
try {
    $files=@(& git -c core.quotepath=false ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard)
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'Generate this reference from a Git checkout.' }
    $files=@(($files + 'docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html') | Sort-Object -Unique)
    # Local-only project memory is deliberately excluded even if accidentally staged.
    $files=@($files | Where-Object { $_ -notin @('AGENT.md','AGENTS.md') })
    $version=(Get-Content -LiteralPath VERSION -Raw).Trim()
    $cards=[Text.StringBuilder]::new(); $symbols=0
    foreach ($file in $files) {
        if (-not $fileHelp.ContainsKey($file)) { throw "Add a public-file description before generating: $file" }
        $id=Get-ReferenceId $file
        $null=$cards.Append('<article data-entry id="'+$id+'"><h2>'+ (ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $file)+'</h2><p>'+(ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $fileHelp[$file])+'</p>')
        $source=if ($file -eq 'docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html') { '' } else { [IO.File]::ReadAllText((Join-Path $root $file)).Replace("`r`n","`n") }
        $functions=@()
        if ($file -match '\.ps(m)?1$') {
            $tokens=$null; $parseErrors=$null
            $ast=[Management.Automation.Language.Parser]::ParseInput($source,[ref]$tokens,[ref]$parseErrors)
            if ($parseErrors.Count) { throw "PowerShell parse error in $file" }
            $functions=@($ast.FindAll({param($node) $node -is [Management.Automation.Language.FunctionDefinitionAst]},$true) | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{Name=$_.Name; Line=$_.Extent.StartLineNumber} })
        } elseif ($file -match '\.cs$') {
            $functions=@([regex]::Matches($source,'(?m)^\s*(?:public|private|internal)\s+static\s+[\w\[\]]+\s+(\w+)\(') | ForEach-Object { [pscustomobject]@{Name=$_.Groups[1].Value; Line=($source.Substring(0,$_.Index) -split "`n").Count} })
        } elseif ($file -match '\.(html|js)$') {
            $functions=@([regex]::Matches($source,'(?m)^\s*(?:function\s+(\w+)\(|const\s+(\w+)\s*=\s*(?:\(\)|\w+)\s*=>)') | ForEach-Object {
                [pscustomobject]@{Name=$(if($_.Groups[1].Success){$_.Groups[1].Value}else{$_.Groups[2].Value}); Line=($source.Substring(0,$_.Index) -split "`n").Count}
            })
        }
        $includeSource=$file -match '\.(ps1|psm1|cs|xaml|sh|bat|json|csv|yml|js|manifest)$' -or $file -in @('.gitignore','.gitattributes','VERSION')
        foreach ($fn in $functions) {
            if (-not $functionHelp.ContainsKey($fn.Name)) { throw "Add a function explanation: $file / $($fn.Name)" }
            $symbols++
            $null=$cards.Append('<div class="symbol"><h3><code>'+(ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $fn.Name)+'</code></h3><p>'+(ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $functionHelp[$fn.Name])+'</p><small>Declared at line '+$fn.Line+'</small></div>')
        }
        if (-not $functions.Count) { $null=$cards.Append('<p class="muted">No named functions: behavior is defined by top-level statements, configuration, markup or prose. See the file purpose above.</p>') }
        if ($includeSource) {
            $null=$cards.Append('<details><summary>Read the full source ('+($source -split "`n").Count+' lines)</summary><pre><code>'+(ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $source)+'</code></pre></details>')
        } elseif ($file -ne 'docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html') {
            $url='../'+$file
            $null=$cards.Append('<p><a href="'+(ConvertTo-ReferenceHtml $url)+'">Open this document</a></p>')
        }
        $null=$cards.Append('</article>')
    }
    $template=@'
<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>LMS Unpacker — every public file and function</title><style>
*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:#f5f4ee;color:#18332f;font:16px/1.65 system-ui,Segoe UI,sans-serif}header{padding:40px max(24px,calc((100vw - 1060px)/2));background:#173e34;color:white}header a{color:#d5f8e7}h1{font-size:clamp(28px,4vw,44px);line-height:1.2}main{max-width:1108px;margin:auto;padding:24px}h2{font-size:23px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}h3{font-size:18px;margin:0 0 6px}a{color:#075d4c}a:focus-visible,input:focus-visible,summary:focus-visible{outline:3px solid #a24200;outline-offset:3px}label{font-weight:700;display:block}input{font:inherit;width:100%;padding:12px;margin:8px 0;border:1px solid #73887f;border-radius:6px}.search{background:#f5f4ee;padding:12px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #cbd6d0}article{border:1px solid #cbd6d0;background:white;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;margin:24px 0;scroll-margin-top:20px}.symbol{border-left:3px solid #a8cdb9;padding:12px 18px;margin:16px 0;background:#f4f8f5}.symbol p{margin:5px 0}code{font-family:Consolas,monospace;overflow-wrap:anywhere;font-size:.9em}small,.muted{color:#48645e}summary{cursor:pointer;font-weight:650;padding:12px 0}pre{background:#18332f;color:#eaf5f0;padding:18px;overflow:auto;line-height:1.5;font-size:13px;border-radius:8px;max-height:650px}pre code{overflow-wrap:normal;font-size:inherit}[hidden]{display:none!important}footer{padding:30px;text-align:center;color:#48645e}@media print{.search{display:none}pre{max-height:none;white-space:pre-wrap;background:#eee;color:black}article{break-inside:auto}header{background:white;color:black}header a{color:black}}
</style></head><body><header><p>LMS Unpacker · __VERSION__ · Source companion</p><h1>Every public file.<br>Every named function.</h1><p>__FILES__ files · __SYMBOLS__ named functions/methods · Offline source snapshots</p><p><a href="BUILD-IT-YOURSELF.html">Read the illustrated handbook first</a> · <a href="../START-HERE.html">User tutorial</a></p></header><main><div class="search"><label for="search">Find a file, function or behavior</label><input id="search" type="search" placeholder="Try CRC, workspace, Invoke-LmsArchive or release" autocomplete="off"><p id="count" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p></div><p>Each file card explains its responsibility. Function entries explain inputs, outputs, side effects and failure behavior. Expand implementation files to read complete source, including event handlers and top-level code. File search includes source text. Ignored local notes and generated binaries are excluded.</p><div id="entries">__CARDS__</div><noscript>Search needs JavaScript; every file and function is still readable below.</noscript></main><footer>Generated by tools/Build-CodeReference.ps1. Update annotations and regenerate after changing source. Source text is HTML-escaped; no external assets or analytics.</footer><script>
'use strict';
const entries=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-entry]'));
const input=document.getElementById('search');
input.addEventListener('input',()=>{const query=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();let count=0;for(const entry of entries){entry.hidden=!entry.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(query);if(!entry.hidden)count++;}document.getElementById('count').textContent=count?count+' files match. Expand a source block to inspect implementation.':'No matches. Try a shorter function name or a behavior such as undo.';});
document.getElementById('count').textContent=entries.length+' files. Search by name or behavior.';
</script></body></html>
'@
    $output=$template.Replace('__VERSION__',$version).Replace('__FILES__',[string]$files.Count).Replace('__SYMBOLS__',[string]$symbols).Replace('__CARDS__',$cards.ToString())
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText((Join-Path $root 'docs/CODE-REFERENCE.html'),$output.Replace("`r`n","`n"),[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false))
    Write-Host "Generated reference: $($files.Count) files, $symbols named functions/methods."
} finally { Pop-Location }

tools/Clean-Workspace.ps1

Preview-first generated-clutter cleanup. Targets only known scratch/old release patterns, verifies current artifacts before removing older packages, checks containment and refuses links.

Assert-CleanupPath

Cleanup guard. Resolves a candidate inside the repository, checks ancestors and walks directory children one level at a time to reject links before recursion. Returns only a validated absolute target.

Declared at line 14
Read the full source (57 lines)
#requires -Version 5.1
<# .SYNOPSIS
Preview known generated clutter; pass -Apply to remove it. Unknown files are untouched.
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param([switch]$Apply)
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$root=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath((Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent))
$prefix=$root.TrimEnd('\','/')+[IO.Path]::DirectorySeparatorChar
$version=(Get-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $root 'VERSION') -Raw).Trim()
if ($version -notmatch '^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$') { throw 'Invalid VERSION.' }
$dist=Join-Path $root 'dist'
$keep=@('LMS-Unpacker.exe','LMS-Unpacker-CLI.exe','LMS-Unpacker-CLI.sh',"LMS-Unpacker-$version.zip")
function Assert-CleanupPath([string]$Path) {
    $resolved=[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($Path)
    if (-not $resolved.StartsWith($prefix,[StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { throw "Outside workspace: $resolved" }
    $cursor=$resolved
    while ($cursor -and $cursor -ne $root) {
        if ((Test-Path -LiteralPath $cursor) -and ((Get-Item -LiteralPath $cursor -Force).Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint)) { throw "Refusing linked path: $cursor" }
        $cursor=[IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($cursor)
    }
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $resolved -PathType Container) {
        # Inspect one level at a time, rejecting links before descending.
        $pending=[Collections.Generic.Stack[string]]::new(); $pending.Push($resolved)
        while ($pending.Count) {
            foreach ($item in Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $pending.Pop() -Force) {
                if ($item.Attributes -band [IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) { throw "Refusing linked child: $($item.FullName)" }
                if ($item.PSIsContainer) { $pending.Push($item.FullName) }
            }
        }
    }
    return $resolved
}
$targets=@()
$scratch=Join-Path $root 'tests/.runs'
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $scratch) { $targets+=Assert-CleanupPath $scratch }
$targets+=@(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $root -File -Filter 'test-results*.txt' | ForEach-Object { Assert-CleanupPath $_.FullName })
if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dist) {
    $null=Assert-CleanupPath $dist
    $old=@(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dist -File | Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^LMS-Unpacker-(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)\.zip(?:\.sha256)?$' -and [version]$Matches[1] -lt [version]$version })
    if ($old.Count) {
        foreach ($name in $keep) {
            $artifact=Join-Path $dist $name; $sidecar=$artifact+'.sha256'
            if (-not [IO.File]::Exists($artifact) -or -not [IO.File]::Exists($sidecar)) { throw 'Build all current artifacts before removing older packages.' }
            $expected=((Get-Content -LiteralPath $sidecar -Raw).Trim() -split '\s+')[0]
            if ($expected -notmatch '^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$' -or (Get-FileHash -LiteralPath $artifact -Algorithm SHA256).Hash -ne $expected) { throw "Current artifact checksum mismatch: $name" }
        }
        $targets+=@($old | ForEach-Object { Assert-CleanupPath $_.FullName })
    }
}
foreach ($target in $targets) {
    if ($Apply) { $null=Assert-CleanupPath $target; Remove-Item -LiteralPath $target -Recurse -Force; Write-Host "Removed: $target" }
    else { Write-Host "Would remove: $target" }
}
if (-not $targets.Count) { Write-Host 'No known generated clutter found.' }
elseif (-not $Apply) { Write-Host 'Preview only. Close active builds/tests, then use -Apply to clean these paths.' }
